Risks: The triage's file list leaves out src/features/bom/page.test.ts:117 (a hard-coded '+€19,180 per crane' that will fail) and src/server/dal/delivery4.golden.json (regenerated by proto-golden-d4.ts, which also uses applyE2a). It also leaves out comments in src/features/bom/i18n.ts and model.test.ts and the formula in spec 09 §1.8. · E-2b only defines the equal-quantity case. The rule qtyTo × new − qtyFrom × old for a substitution whose quantity also changes is a default to record in the delivery notes and DECISIONS follow-up. · If an old or new ref is missing from the catalogue, projectPartValuation leaves it out. Treating a missing old price as 0 would charge the full new price. Fall back to the quantity-only rule or show '—', and test that case. · The old part is valued at the project's ordered price when it sits on an approved order. On HLT-026, if someone approves PO-1082 (FD-DET-OPT × 180, now 'Awaiting approval') in the running app, the old part takes its ordered price. The seed order lines carry the catalogue price, so the displayed value does not change, but tests that approve orders should be checked. · The prototype's delta at line 4266 has to be patched in the proto-e2a style (each swap() fails loudly if its text is missing). An error in the patch would silently shift both goldens: compare the regenerated JSON diff by hand, only the two substitution rows and the total should change. · The tooltip is a title attribute, so it does not show on touch tablets (R-146 layouts). It must stay hidden when prices are masked (the `prices` flag / PRICE_MASK for the client and roles without price rights). · revision_diffs and revision_diff_lines have no writer (seed only). The change is visible only on the seeded HLT-026 ASM-RMG-250 B → C comparison until diffs come from PDM or ECOs. This does not block the item, but the delivery notes should say so.

#### R-108 — P&ID : rechercher l'article d'un repère dans tout le catalogue (buildable, M)

Same as proposed, with these fixes.
(1) Pure rule `searchPidCandidates` in src/domain/pid.ts: text search on reference or name, type filter (default: the tag's compatible types, or « Tous les types »), project BOM articles first and marked, then catalogue order, at most 20 results plus a « refine » flag. It replaces PID_MAX_CANDIDATES and pidCandidatesOf.
(2) URL state `cq` / `ctype` in src/features/pid/links.ts (parse and build), read by the page. Reuse the screen's existing `?q=` search pattern.
(3) DAL pid.ts:
- `pidCandidates` takes the query and type and searches the whole catalogue (54 parts in the seed).
- `mapPidTag` accepts any catalogue reference. It still refuses an unknown reference, a client tag and the reference already mapped.
- On request, in the same transaction, it adds the BOM line through addBomLine refactored to take an optional tx. Quantity is 1 by default, scope is the tag's (keystone or overlay).
- Activity is the tag's activityCode ONLY if it is in the project plan, else null. Imported tags may carry codes that are not in the plan (src/domain/pid-import.ts:338 only warns), and addBomLine would throw « Unknown activity ».
- The DAL also re-checks the `bom` workspace.
(4) Server Function: mapPidTagAction drops the candidate pre-check (actions.ts:62-64), takes an `addToBom` flag and a quantity, and checks ctx.canEdit('bom') when the line is added. It writes audit and activity on the tag and the BOM, then revalidatePath on /projects/[code] (layout) and screenHref('plm'). Update the KNOWN error list.
(5) UI:
- Search field and type select in the tag drawer.
- The candidate row shows « Dans la nomenclature » or the part status.
- An out-of-BOM pick opens a client confirmation with a quantity: « Lier et ajouter à la nomenclature » (only if canEdit('bom')) or « Lier seulement », which leaves the tag Not in BOM and keeps the existing « Add reference to project BOM » link.
- Replace the hint « Candidates are BOM articles of a compatible type ».
- French strings go in src/i18n/extra (new d8 JSON or pid.json), not only in the feature i18n.ts.
(6) Tests:
- Domain rule.
- Actions: personas, read-only project, unknown reference, client tag, BOM duplicate, activity not in plan.
- Also update the existing tests the scope's file list misses: src/features/pid/page.test.ts:81 (expects 8 candidates), src/server/dal/delivery5.test.ts:509-512 (golden candidates per drawer), src/server/dal/delivery5-writes.test.ts:381 (KS-STL-0450 refused as "not a BOM article of a compatible type" becomes allowed), src/domain/pid.test.ts:73 and render.test.ts:132.
- Keep src/features/pid/pid.golden.json as generated by scripts/proto-golden-pid.ts. Change the test assertions, with comments citing E-6, instead of hand-editing the generated golden file.

Risks: The proposed file list misses existing tests that will fail: src/features/pid/page.test.ts (expects 8 candidates for TT-102), src/server/dal/delivery5.test.ts (compares pidCandidates with golden drawers), src/server/dal/delivery5-writes.test.ts (expects KS-STL-0450 to be refused), and possibly src/server/dal/delivery4-writes.test.ts if addBomLine's signature changes. · pid.golden.json is generated from the prototype by scripts/proto-golden-pid.ts. Hand-editing it makes it drift from the generator. The project rule is to update the test with a comment citing the decision id. · Tag activity codes can sit outside the project plan (the import only warns), and addBomLine throws « Unknown activity ». Without a fallback to null, the whole « Lier et ajouter » transaction would fail. · bomRefs counts only lines with quantity > 0 (src/server/dal/pid.ts:60-64). A BOM line with quantity 0, possible through the interchange import, shows the tag as Not in BOM, and adding the line then hits the duplicate refusal « already in the project BOM ». It needs a clear message, or an update of the quantity instead. · A-1b (who masters the article catalogue, possibly SolidWorks 3DX) is still « À discuter ». The search must read the local catalogue only and must never offer to create an article, or it would pre-empt that decision. · Obsolete, Outdated and Not validated articles stay selectable, as in the prototype's candidates. Linking them gives the tag a reference issue (part_obsolete and so on). This is a default to report in the delivery notes. · Searching on every keystroke through the URL with the drawer as a Server Component needs a debounced client input (router.replace) or a GET form, so the drawer is not re-rendered on each key. · The 20-result cap with « Affinez la recherche » and the default quantity 1 are defaults that E-6 does not state. List them as deviations in the delivery notes. · Writing the BOM from the P&ID screen must also call the DAL-level `bom` workspace check (requireWorkspace) and record audit and activity on both objects, following the Server Function convention.

#### R-109 — P&ID : signaler l'écart de révision avec Codex et proposer un ré-import (buildable, S)

Le périmètre est celui du triage, avec quatre précisions.

1. Règle pure dans src/domain/pid.ts : pidRevisionGap(sheetRevision, codexRevision, codexStatus). Elle renvoie { ahead: boolean, referenceRevision } en réutilisant la règle E-5 déjà en place dans engineering.ts (inputLatest / isReferenceStatus), plutôt que d'ignorer tout brouillon Codex. Quand Codex est en brouillon (Draft), la référence est la révision précédente, via prevRev. Ainsi, « Codex rév. E Draft » face à des repères en rév. C signale bien la rév. D. Le statut obsolète est exclu explicitement, avec un commentaire, puisque REFERENCE_STATUSES l'inclut. L'ordre des révisions doit rester cohérent avec nextRevision : A…Y, puis Z → Z1, et les nombres en numérique. Une paire non comparable, par exemple « C1 » face à « D », ne déclenche pas le bandeau « en retard ». Elle donne au plus un libellé neutre « différente ». On évite ainsi un faux « Codex a une révision plus récente » quand l'ingénierie a importé une révision que Codex n'a pas encore diffusée.

2. getPidValidation expose sheet.codexAhead (ou gap), dans src/server/dal/pid.ts. pidScreenView expose drawing.gap : texte traduit, révisions passées par revT, statut passé par documentStatus.label. Le bandeau est un Callout en tête de PidBody, visible de tous ceux qui voient l'écran. Il n'apparaît pas quand le P&ID n'a pas de fiche Codex (documentId nul).

3. Pour les éditeurs du P&ID seulement (ctx.canEdit('pid'), donc pas sur un projet en lecture seule), le bandeau porte le bouton « Importer les repères P&ID ». Il ouvre ImportTagsDialog avec une nouvelle prop suggestedRevision qui vaut la révision de référence Codex, au lieu de nextRevision. Le bouton d'en-tête existant reste et reçoit la même suggestion. Rien n'est bloqué : étapes, validation et info-bulle sont inchangées.

4. Tests et documentation, en citant A-9 :
   - src/domain/pid.test.ts : ordre des révisions, brouillon, obsolète, révisions non comparables ;
   - render.test.ts : nouvelles chaînes, en écart déclaré par rapport au prototype ;
   - page.test.ts : bandeau sur HLT-026 en EN et FR, bouton seulement pour les éditeurs ;
   - mise à jour de docs/reste-a-developper.json selon la règle permanente.

Risks: Tension avec le processus : la dernière étape vise nextRevision de la révision des repères (releasedStepLabel : « released for construction (rev D) » pour des repères en rév. C). L'OQ13 de la spec 09 avait déjà relevé que Codex était à « rév. D Released » alors que l'étape 5 attend encore la rév. D. Après chaque diffusion pour construction réussie, Codex aura donc une révision d'avance sur les repères et le bandeau s'allumera systématiquement. Valeur par défaut à décider et à documenter : soit on l'accepte (le propriétaire a présenté exactement le seed comme le cas d'écart), soit on masque le bandeau quand l'étape de diffusion est « done » pour la révision Codex. · Le périmètre du triage dit qu'un brouillon Codex ne déclenche jamais le bandeau. C'est plus étroit que la règle E-5 (inputLatest : brouillon → révision précédente comme référence) et cela manquerait un écart réel (Codex rév. E Draft, rév. D déjà diffusée, repères en rév. C). Il faut réutiliser inputLatest / prevRev. · L'ordre proposé « Z < AA » contredit la convention existante de nextRevision (Z → Z1). L'import accepte aussi des révisions libres (normaliseRevision : 1 à 6 caractères alphanumériques). Un repli « différent » qui déclencherait le bandeau « en retard » produirait des faux positifs quand les repères viennent d'une révision plus récente ou non comparable. · L'exclusion du statut obsolète s'écarte volontairement de REFERENCE_STATUSES, qui l'inclut : il faut l'écrire en commentaire avec A-9 / E-5. · La révision Codex est le miroir de Keystone (table documents, alimentée par le seed et par le connecteur Codex simulé). Le bandeau reflète la dernière synchronisation, pas Codex en direct, ce qui est acceptable selon le modèle des connecteurs. · render.test.ts compare au texte du prototype : les nouvelles chaînes du bandeau doivent être déclarées comme écarts, et le fichier FR src/features/pid/i18n.ts doit les couvrir. page.test.ts doit vérifier l'absence du bouton pour les non-éditeurs et sur un projet en lecture seule. · Un deuxième ImportTagsButton (en-tête plus bandeau) duplique l'état du dialogue : il faut s'assurer qu'un seul dialogue s'ouvre à la fois, ou faire en sorte que le bouton du bandeau réutilise celui de l'en-tête.

#### R-110 — Ne protéger pour le parc que les pièces réellement montées (buildable, S)

Rewrite installedBasePins (src/server/dal/eco.ts) to read the as-maintained configuration of the targeted machines through machineConfigurations(numbers) (src/server/dal/fleet.ts:372), joining machines.number to targetRef. If possible, put the filter in a pure function in src/domain/eco.ts with a test in eco.test.ts. A machine with a recorded configuration pins only the ECO refs it carries, and the pin's revision is the revision fitted on that machine for that ref. A machine with no configuration row (added with "Add machine", or known to Vigie but not to Keystone) keeps the cautious rule and pins every ECO part. Update the comment that cites 09 OQ21. pinSummary and the consumers stay unchanged: the BOM PU/PR list (src/server/dal/bom.ts:358-369), the stock page and drawer (src/features/stock/model.ts:83-87), and the scrap warning. Nothing displays pin.revision today, so that change is data-only. Seed changes: EL-HMI-15 and FD-DET-OPT2 are no longer pinned; ASM-RMG-250, EL-VFD-200 and FD-DET-OPT are unchanged. The goldens are generated from the prototype, so do not regenerate them. Override the expected values in each test, with a comment citing E-4, in: src/domain/stock.test.ts:150-157; src/features/stock/model.test.ts:41-46 (stock rows vs stock.golden.json, FD-DET-OPT2); src/features/bom/model.test.ts:197-212 (spare rows vs bom.golden.json, EL-HMI-15 and FD-DET-OPT2); src/server/dal/delivery4.test.ts:151 (spare list vs golden), 266-269 and 380 (stock). Re-check src/features/stock/render.test.ts:49 and src/features/bom/render.test.ts:65; both strings survive, but their neighbours should be verified. Replace the proposed removal or replacement test with tests that the current code supports: (1) a pinned machine that does not carry an ECO part gets no pin for it; (2) "Mark done" that fits that part (for example EL-HMI-15 on MCH-029) makes the pin appear, with the fitted revision; (3) a machine added with "Add machine", or a target unknown to Keystone, still gets every ECO part. Then update docs/reste-a-developper.json, regenerate the page and record E-4 as applied.

Risks: The configuration is only as good as after-sales keeps it. Nothing can remove a part from a machine's configuration: completeIntervention only updates or inserts. A part physically removed therefore stays pinned, which errs on the safe side but is not exact. · A retrofit that upgrades the part to the ECO's new revision (same ref, new revision) keeps the pin, because the rule tests the ref and not the revision. Dropping the pin once the machine carries the ECO's toRevision would be an extra rule beyond E-4. Leave it out, or note it as an open question, rather than build it. · Treating a machine with no configuration as carrying every ECO part is a default the owner has not confirmed. The alternative would be to mark it 'unknown', as in E-8 option c. List it in the delivery's defaults. · The Vigie mock's effectivity is built from the prototype's AS_MAINT at seed time, not from the database configuration. Machines registered later through 'Register machines' never become ECO targets in the mock, so the filter can only be exercised on the six seeded machines or with a hand-built connector fixture. · The triage's test list misses src/features/stock/model.test.ts and src/features/bom/model.test.ts. Both compare against prototype goldens that still show the old pins, so they will fail unless overridden with E-4 comments. · One more database read joins the hot paths of the stock page and the BOM spare list. The cost is small but the query should be batched: one query for all target machines, not one per ECO.

#### R-111 — Signaler le doublon possible de la grue EQ-CRN-250T / ASM-RMG-250 (buildable, S)

Same as the triage, with these details:
1. Pure rule `possibleDuplicates(lines, childrenOf)` in src/domain/bom.ts. It pairs a purchased line (makeBuy 'purchased') with an assembly line (type 'assembly' or having children), with the same HS code and the same catalogue price, where neither is in the other's structure (walk it with buildAssembly). getProjectBom (src/server/dal/bom.ts:116) exposes it on each BomLine (the partner ref) and in the summary (count).
2. Flat list (src/features/bom/model.ts:177): a note « Possible duplicate of <ref> · to be checked by the engineering office », with a French translation in src/features/bom/i18n.ts. It must sit alongside the existing totalNote, which ASM-RMG-250 already carries.
3. KPI « BOM value » (model.ts:62): a note or banner « 1 possible duplicate · value may be counted twice ». Do not state an amount: EQ-CRN-250T is valued at the ordered price, ASM-RMG-250 through its components.
4. Part drawer: PartDrawer (src/features/plm/part-drawer.tsx, partDrawerView in plm/model.ts) is shared by the catalogue, stock, P&ID and BOM, and getPartDrawer has no BOM context. Add a small DAL helper and show the note only when a projectCode is given, or per project the part is used in.
5. Optional, for consistency: the same note in the KPI sheet of the BOM export (src/server/export/providers/engineering.ts:178).
6. No change to the BOM value or the order value, and no dismiss button.
7. Tests: the pure rule in src/domain/bom.test.ts, delivery4.test.ts, render.test.ts and page.test.ts. Update bom.golden.json and model.test.ts if a new field changes the compared views, with a comment citing NEW-D4-5.

Risks: The rule is a guess from the data. If someone edits the catalogue price or HS code of either part (PLM, table import from X3), the flag disappears without any answer from the bureau d'études. A new purchased/assembly pair can raise a false alarm. · If the bureau d'études answers b (two machines), the rule gives no way to clear the flag for this pair. That would take a code exclusion, which is a constant and goes against R-169, or a data mark (migration). Consider a seeded data mark if the owner prefers that. · ASM-RMG-250 is already left out of the BOM value (valued through its components). Any amount shown as « counted twice » would be wrong or ambiguous: €3.70M ordered for EQ-CRN-250T against about €1.44M of components. Keep the wording qualitative. · The ASM-RMG-250 row already carries the note « Valued through its components — not added to the BOM value ». Two notes on one row need layout care, including tablet layouts (R-146). · The shared PartDrawer is used on screens with no project (catalogue, stock). Showing the note there needs a per-project wording, or nothing at all. · It interacts with the open cost-control question on PO-1052 list prices (orders placed €3.95M on HLT-026). Do not mix the two corrections. · Priority is « plus tard » (later). The flag only shows on demo data (HLT-026). A production seed without project BOMs flags nothing. · Adding fields to FlatRowView or KpiView may break the deep-equal golden comparisons (bom.golden.json). Update them with a comment citing NEW-D4-5.

#### R-145 — Coût garantie sur l'année civile et alerte sur la fin prolongée (NEW-D5-2 « a ») (buildable, S)

The scope stands as proposed, with these small corrections. 1) savKpis(claims, ivs, lang, today = NOW_ISO). The "Warranty cost YTD" value and its "n chargeable" sub-line count only claims whose reportedOn is in today's calendar year. Match on reportedOn.slice(0,4) === today.slice(0,4) (or a range from 1 Jan to today). ClaimFacts gains reportedOn. The other tiles do not change. 2) Add a pure warrantyInForce({ warrantyEndOn, extendedUntil, warrantyStatus }, today = NOW_ISO) in src/domain/aftersales.ts that returns { status, endOn }. The status is 'extended' only when extendedUntil is set, is later than warrantyEndOn (or there is no warrantyEndOn) and is ≥ today. It is 'in_warranty' while endOn ≥ today, and 'out_of_warranty' once an end date has passed. With no dates, it falls back to the stored status. addedWarrantyStatus becomes a thin wrapper around it. 3) In listMachines (fleet.ts:115-125), warrantyStatus, warrantyText, warrantyEndingSoon and warrantyEndsInDays come from that computed state. fleetKpis uses the end in force: add extendedUntil to MachineFacts, or read the row's computed fields. Everything is computed on read, with no migration and no job. The "Warranty end" column keeps the contractual date. 4) Optional French label change: "Warranty cost YTD" becomes "Coût garantie de l'année" in a d8 extra dictionary, or directly in fr-dict.json. 5) Tests. src/domain/aftersales.test.ts: the claims fixture takes reportedOn from column 4 of each P.CLAIMS row. The `extra` WC-020 literal at :186 also needs reportedOn ('2026-09-30'). Add the NEW-D5-2 cases: a 2025 claim excluded from the value and the sub-line; today = 2027-01-05 gives 0; an extension ending within 3 months is counted as ending soon; an expired extension is Out of warranty; a contractual end just passed is Out of warranty. Add a DAL case with an inserted machine (past extendedUntil) and a 2025 claim, placed in delivery5-writes.test.ts (or on its own fresh database) so the per-zone goldens in delivery5.test.ts are not affected. Update the FR label in model.test.ts, citing NEW-D5-2. 6) Docs: delivery-8 notes, reste-a-developper.json (R-145 progress, history, updated), then npm run remaining:page and republish the page. Out of scope: any form for recording a warranty extension. Extensions keep coming from the seed or data until the owner validates such a form.

Risks: Two clocks: warrantyEnding still compares month indexes against PG_NOW = 8.97, while the status and the year filter use NOW_ISO = '2026-09-30'. They agree today (monthIndexOfIso('2026-09-30') = 8.967), but both must move together when R-013 brings the real clock. Until then the tile never resets on 1 January, because 'today' is frozen at 30 Sep 2026. · French label override: no extra dictionary overrides a fr-dict.json entry today, and fr-dict.json has not changed since deliveries 1-2. dictionaries.test.ts only checks the `d7-` prefix. If delivery 8 extends it to `d8-`, overriding 'Warranty cost YTD' will show up as a clash with fr-dict.json. To avoid that, exempt the key explicitly, or edit fr-dict.json and cite NEW-D5-2. The relabel is also a cosmetic change the owner did not explicitly ask for, so it can be dropped. · Required reportedOn on ClaimFacts breaks the typecheck of the inline `extra` ClaimFacts literal in aftersales.test.ts:186, which the proposed test list does not mention. · Inconsistent extension data (extendedUntil earlier than or equal to warrantyEndOn) would read 'Extended → <earlier date>' unless 'extended' requires extendedUntil > warrantyEndOn. The seed is unaffected; there is no writer for extendedUntil other than the seed. · If the YTD filter uses an upper bound reportedOn <= today, a claim dated after the frozen business date (from an import or a test) would silently disappear. Matching on the calendar year alone avoids this. · Inserting a machine and a claim inside delivery5.test.ts, a shared read-only golden file, could perturb the per-zone golden expectations, depending on order. Put the case in delivery5-writes.test.ts or on a fresh database. · The 'Extended' status while the contractual end has not passed yet is a default with no prototype precedent: the prototype's only extended machine is past its contractual end. It should be listed as a default in the delivery-8 notes.

#### R-155 — Corriger le message de zone vide des listes de machines (buildable, S)

Add a machine-specific notice to the after-sales view model (src/features/aftersales/model.ts) and leave the shared project notice alone.

On the after-sales screens:
- Installed base: when fleetZoneEmpty is true, show t('No machine in this zone').
- Warranty & claims: keep the savZoneEmpty rule (07 A.1) for when the notice appears, and pick the text from data.machines, which is already filtered by zone:
  - no machine in the zone: t('No machine in this zone'), as the owner's X-16 text says;
  - machines in the zone but the current view's list is empty (seed case APAC › Interventions): a text that names that view's list, for example 'No claim in this zone', 'No intervention in this zone' and 'No warranty cost in this zone' (FR « Aucune réclamation / Aucune intervention / Aucun coût de garantie dans cette zone »);
  - record this as a deliberate deviation citing X-16 and 07 A.1 in the delivery-8 notes.
  - Fallback: if you want only the owner's exact words, ask the owner first, or apply the machine text only when the zone has no machine.

Dictionary: add the FR entries to src/i18n/extra/aftersales.json, including "No machine in this zone": "Aucune machine dans cette zone".

Project screens (Projects register, Portfolio overview, Portfolio schedule, World map, Prioritisation, Cash) keep « Aucun projet dans cette zone pour cette vue ».

Tests, each with a comment citing X-16:
- page.test.ts:114 and render.test.ts:87 (APAC › Interventions) expect the interventions text.
- render.test.ts:54 (fleetView with no machines, apac, FR) expects « Aucune machine dans cette zone ».
- render.test.ts:48 checks that « Aucune machine dans cette zone » is absent.
- model.test.ts:83-84 expect the new texts instead of G[lang].fleet.all.zoneEmptyText. Add a sav case where a zone has no machine at all, and optionally change model.test.ts:43.

No change to fleetZoneEmpty / savZoneEmpty, the golden JSON files or the domain tests. Then update docs/reste-a-developper.json (R-155 progress) and regenerate the remaining-work page, as CLAUDE.md requires.

Risks: The proposed default ("No machine in this zone" on every Warranty & claims view) would be false for APAC › Interventions in the seed, where the zone has 1 machine and 1 claim but no intervention. That case is pinned by page.test.ts:114 and render.test.ts:87, so the tests would lock in a wrong message. · Per-view texts go slightly beyond the owner's literal X-16 wording. Record them as a deviation (X-16 + 07 A.1), or get a one-line owner confirmation; otherwise reviewers may see them as unapproved wording. · If someone also changes the golden zoneEmptyText in src/domain/aftersales.golden.json, or edits the proto-golden script, the prototype comparison breaks. That golden must stay the prototype's text; only the view-model tests should move. · render.test.ts:48 (a not.toContain check on the old French text) would still pass after the change but would no longer test anything; it must check the new text. · Changing the shared key 'No project in this zone for this view' in src/i18n/fr-dict.json, instead of adding a new key, would also change the six project screens (and the pfplan/pfinance/worldmap/portfolio tests that use it).

#### R-153 — Retirer le nom du projet des formulaires qui ne visent pas un projet (buildable, S)

Remove the context prop, and the getLastProject/getProject loads plus imports that become unused, from these pages: src/app/(app)/admin/machine-types/page.tsx (New machine type; listProjects stays because machineTypesView needs it), src/app/(app)/reference/parts/page.tsx (New part), src/app/(app)/after-sales/fleet/page.tsx (Add machine; listProjects stays for the leads set) and src/app/(app)/after-sales/claims/page.tsx (New claim and Plan intervention; treating Plan intervention the same way is correct, since planInterventionAction(null, …) records nothing on a project). Sync vault (src/app/(app)/reference/drawings/page.tsx) should NOT simply lose the project name, because Scope = "Current project" attaches the run to getLastProject(). Either keep its sub-line, as on New RFQ, or move the project into the option label, for example "Current project · #026 Harbor Line…", via projectLabelText (R-148) in syncVaultForm, then drop the sub-line. The second option follows X-10 more closely. Keep the sub-line on New RFQ (sourcing), What-if and every /projects/[code]/* page. Update the comments that still say "as every add-form of the prototype (projectName)": the file headers of fleet/page.tsx and parts/page.tsx, machine-types/page.tsx:23, src/features/admin/primary-buttons.tsx:8, src/features/plm/plm-buttons.tsx:13 and src/features/aftersales/add-buttons.tsx:16. Update the tests, citing X-10: src/features/aftersales/page.test.ts:49-61 (the EN and FR context assertions on Add machine), src/features/plm/page.test.ts:82 and :138, src/features/admin/page.test.ts:96, and src/features/eco/page.test.ts:136 only if the Sync vault sub-line is replaced by an option label. No end-to-end test asserts these sub-lines. Then update docs/reste-a-developper.json and regenerate the page as CLAUDE.md requires.

Risks: Sync vault: removing the sub-line without a replacement hides which project 'Current project' means on a portfolio-wide screen, even though the run is attached to that project (eco/actions.ts:104). This goes against X-10's own reason for the change. Keep it, or move it into the option label. · ESLint unused-import failures if getLastProject, getProject or projectLabelText are left imported after the context is removed. machine-types still needs listProjects, and fleet still needs listProjects and projectAccessResolver. · The session mocks in the page tests still provide getLastProject. That is harmless, but the aftersales and plm tests also switch session.project to NDC-031 to check the FR sub-line, so those assertions need rewriting, not just deleting. · Stale code comments and spec references (01 OQ14, 06 OQ14, 09 §0.3 'every add-form names the current project') would contradict the new behaviour unless they are updated or annotated with X-10. · Priority is 'plus tard' (X-10 Later). It is buildable, but low value for delivery 8.

## `connectors` — Vigie and Cursus connectors on the Keystone side: mirror pruning and contract, project-filtered links, rejected-receipt NCR link and alerts, dormant Vigie notification hooks, Cursus staff directory, Cursus event endpoint, absences from Cursus and GDPR minimisation of leave

Items: R-023, R-024, R-025, R-096, R-015, R-027, R-029, R-031, R-173. Depends on lots: none. Migration: yes.

COMMON (all lots): start from main-2a2fep after the integrator's preparatory commit; read docs/ARCHITECTURE.md, the decisions cited (docs/DECISIONS.md) and the CURRENT code (inventory line numbers may have moved). EN source strings, FR only in your src/i18n/extra/d8-<lot>.json (never fr-dict.json or index.ts); typed text via dataText / projectLabelText / fieldsText, never t(). Rights via getProjectContext / projectAccessResolver; each Server Function validates input and permission server-side, writes through DAL writers in one transaction (re-read state inside it, return values from the callback), audits, records activity, revalidatePath. Schema: one `npm run db:generate` migration (+ one `drizzle-kit generate --custom` data migration for R-031); never edit an existing migration. Notifications: createNotification({ toPersonIds }) after commit, actor removed, try/catch + logError. Never UPDATE/DELETE audit_entries. Never modify the sibling Vigie/Cursus/Codex projects: everything they must do goes in the contract documents. Tests cite decision ids; prototype goldens are not regenerated (override with a comment); new tests in new files; loop over PERSONAS instead of counting 8. Never edit docs/reste-a-developper.json: write docs/delivery-8/notes-connectors.md. Insert new code next to related code, never at the end of a shared file. Hand over green: typecheck, lint, test, db:check.

R-023 Vigie connector, Keystone side (V-2 a, V-3a a). In upsertMirror (vigie/mirror.ts:55-59) and upsertNcrMirror (quality-mirror.ts:42-46), when prune is set, delete a gone row only if it has no outbox entry whose syncStatus is not 'sent', the condition INSIDE the DELETE (WHERE id IN gone AND NOT EXISTS an undelivered vigie_eco_progress / vigie_ncr_progress row); no migration. Tests: delivery fails and the refresh no longer lists the ECO or NCR → the entry survives and is redelivered by redeliverEcoOutbox / redeliverNcrOutbox; an empty refresh keeps rows with undelivered entries and prunes the others; delivery5-writes.test.ts:123 still holds. Loosen NCR_NUMBER in src/features/quality/actions.ts:43 (used at :87 and :116; touch only this constant) to a safe generic reference pattern accepting both NCR-029 and Vigie's own numbers (e.g. EV-2026-130), with a test; the VigieNcrRecord.number comment (types.ts:156) says it is Vigie's number. Write docs/integrations/vigie-contract.md (a draft Keystone ↔ Vigie interface contract built from the existing types and endpoints): GET /api/ecos | /api/ncrs | /api/hse ?source=keystone, POST /api/ecos/:number/progress and /api/ncrs/:project/:number/progress, the VigieEcoRecord / VigieNcrRecord / VigieHseRecord fields, number = Vigie's number, vigieRef = Vigie id, keystoneId idempotency, the { id } answer, the provisional X-Api-Key header, closed records stay listed; project-key section explicitly 'waiting for NEW-SYS-1 / R-022'; /signaler parameters referenced (R-025, R-096). Open question in notes: refuse new progress on rows Vigie no longer lists.

R-024 ECO section of the same contract (NEW-VIGIE-1 a, NEW-D4-1 a): routes /modifications-techniques, /modifications-techniques/<ECO number>, /modifications-techniques/nouvelle with part, project, title, source=keystone; register filters part and project (English names as agreed, unlike Vigie's French ones); GET /api/ecos?source=keystone returning VigieEcoRecord[] (types.ts:9-47) with statuses (draft, impact_analysis, in_approval, released, rejected), approval keys (englead, quality, pm; the director holds none), decisions and dispositions as in codes.ts; POST progress with the body of eco-connector.ts:111-114 answered by { id }, plus a PROPOSED stable actor id / e-mail (Entra); refresh at most once a minute with full-refresh semantics. Marked placeholders: authentication (V-3), project identifier (NEW-SYS-1), progress on ECOs no longer listed, an optional NOT agreed claim-number prefill, long free-text titles accepted, and an optional partStatusChanges field (V-2 proposal, used by R-102 later). links.ts: replace the five 'OPEN QUESTION V-1' markers with a note that the routes and parameters were agreed by NEW-D4-1 'a' (Vigie adopts them); rename the describe title at delivery4-writes.test.ts:304; no URL or assertion change. R-024 stays en_cours (Vigie's module awaited).

R-025 project filter on Vigie lists (V-3b a). vigieNcrListUrl, vigieHseListUrl and vigieCoactivityUrl (and the fallback list links of vigieHseUrl) take an optional { projectCode } sent as 'projet' through ONE helper in links.ts (a later NEW-SYS-1 mapping changes a single place); pass r.projectCode from quality-mirror.ts:137; reword (do not delete) the V-3 comment at links.ts:66 and the section note: the value is the Keystone code until NEW-SYS-1 / R-022. Pass `code` at src/features/site/model.ts:204 — edit only that call (the site-quality lot owns the rest of the file); update src/features/site/render.test.ts:118 and add link assertions in delivery5-writes.test.ts:95-100. No new button. Re-exports in dal/quality.ts and dal/site.ts unchanged. R-025 stays en_cours.

R-096 rejected receipt → prefilled Vigie supplier NCR and buyer alert (NEW-D4-3 a, SC-3b a, V-3b a). links.ts: vigieReportNcrUrl gains supplier, supplierCode, order, receipt, quantity → fournisseur, code_fournisseur, commande, reception, quantite next to type=NC, projet, article, titre, source=keystone (documented as a V-3 proposal; empty values dropped by qs()); tests in a new src/server/integrations/vigie/links.test.ts. loadReceipts (src/server/dal/deliveries.ts) adds suppliers.x3Code to ReceiptRow. Drawer (deliveries model.ts, deliveries-view.tsx): for a Rejected or Partially rejected receipt, under 'Quality check', one 'Declare the supplier NCR in Vigie' link per line with a rejected quantity (suffix '· <ref> × <qty>' when several), VIGIE_LINK_ATTRS, project, part ref, supplier, PO, receipt number, quantity and title; internal viewers only; nothing recorded in Keystone; no NC echo (V-3 question). Alert sent from decide() in src/features/deliveries/actions.ts after the DAL transaction commits (covers row Reject, drawer Reject, Partial rejection and the My approvals inbox path), best effort; use a small DAL reader (PO buyer, rejected total) rather than changing decideReceipt's return type. Recipients (default, owner to confirm): the buyer = submitted_by, else created_by, else the owner of the RFQ that drafted the order, keeping persons with an active user account; plus the project's leads (PM of record, co-PMs, deputies) for X-6a; the actor removed at every step (if the buyer is the actor, do not fall back to someone else); if nobody remains with an active account, the procurement-role users minus the actor through toPersonIds (never audience, which would include the actor). kind 'delivery', title 'Delivery rejected at receipt', sub 'BL-… · PO-… · <supplier> · <n> rejected', screen 'deliveries', params { receipt }, projectCode. Toast on a rejection adds the 'declare the supplier NCR in Vigie' segment on the Deliveries screen only (neutral in the English inbox path), through messageText / a RULES entry in deliveries/i18n.ts ('<n> rejected', suffixed labels). Tests: links (new params, empty dropped); model (links only on rejected lines, none for Accepted/Announced/QC pending); actions (resolution order and fallback; actor excluded incl. buyer = actor; inbox path notifies; a notification failure leaves the QC committed); drawer render. Demos and scenarios use the quality persona as actor (Fabien Bertrand is both the seeded buyer and the procurement persona).

R-015 parts (X-6a): (1) invoice blocked — in the same decide() path, compare matchedInvoiceStatus of the invoices of the receipt or its PO before and after QC; each turning 'awaiting_receipt' → 'blocked' notifies the buyer chain above (else the procurement users) plus the director-role users (prototype n8), actor removed; kind 'invoice'. (2) Dormant hooks, only in the HTTP refreshFromVigie of eco-connector.ts and quality-connector.ts (diff before/after the upsert; never inside upsertMirror / upsertNcrMirror, which the seed calls): a new NCR notifies the project leads plus the quality and englead referents on the project team (fallback: leads); an ECO entering in_approval notifies each pending key holder (pm key → leads of the affected projects via leadProjectCodes; quality and englead keys → users holding those roles). Fixed EN titles, typed text in the sub. Tests with injected records and a stubbed fetch. R-015 stays en_cours (mock mode; KPI alerts deferred).

R-027 Cursus staff directory, Keystone half, dormant (NEW-CURSUS-2 a). Migration: persons.matricule (text, unique, nullable), persons.directory_team (text), persons.directory_synced_at (timestamp) — the roles-rates-hr lot adds persons.rate_role_id next to them. Pure matching in src/domain/directory.ts: matricule first, else lower-case work e-mail among persons with clientId null (external contacts never touched); an e-mail held by more than one person matches nobody and is reported; no match → a new person with a unique shortName derived from the name ('J. Wagner', suffix on collision) and initials; an existing shortName is never renamed; team → department only when the names are exactly equal, else kept in directory_team and logged. Writer importStaffDirectory(entries) in src/server/dal/persons-directory.ts: upsert, audit and activity, never delete, never create users or roles, never overwrite an external contact's e-mail nor create a second person with an existing e-mail. Connector: listDirectory() in cursus/types.ts and connector.ts calling GET /api/integrations/keystone/annuaire with X-Api-Key → [{ matricule, nom, prenom, email, equipe }], TODO(cursus) + a new open question; the mock returns nothing ('not connected'). Switch CURSUS_DIRECTORY (default off) in parse.ts next to the CURSUS_* block and .env.example. Daily 'cursus-directory' job (under your marker) respecting connectorEnabled; the Cursus row's existing 'Sync now' (integrations.ts:184) also runs the import when the switch is on. Use the mapping: recordsOfPayload and the refresh take display names from persons by matricule before the mirror's own names (connector.ts:133-163); upsertClearances sets personId by matricule (mirror.ts:19-23); stored clearance person_name rows are NOT renamed (unique (project_code, person_name) and reminders match by name) — names come from persons at read time. Tests: the matching function (ambiguous e-mail, collisions, external contact), the import, the job with a stubbed fetch.

R-029 endpoint for events pushed by Cursus (NEW-CURSUS-7 a). POST /api/integrations/cursus/events Route Handler with no viewer and no same-origin check; X-Api-Key compared in constant time with a NEW secret CURSUS_EVENTS_KEY (SECRET_VARIABLES in parse.ts + .env.example; required in production; the demo key refused in production; no fallback to CURSUS_API_KEY, which Keystone sends to Cursus); isPublicPath (src/domain/auth.ts) exempts exactly this path, with tests in src/domain/auth and src/proxy.test.ts (Entra mode without a session reaches the route, the key is still required); body limit 256 KB; a new generous 'events' rate-limit bucket keyed by client address (src/server/http/request-guard.ts); 503 + Retry-After when connectorEnabled('Cursus') is false or the database is unavailable; codes 200/400/401/413/429/503. Pure zod envelope { id, type, emis_le, data } in src/domain/cursus-events.ts accepting heures.validees { matricule, semaine, projets: { code: heures } } with an optional lignes[] (proposal for NEW-CURSUS-5 c) and replacement keys. Migration: cursus_events inbox (event id unique → a duplicate answers 200 { duplicate: true }; heures.validees for the same (matricule, semaine) replaces the previous event in one transaction; absence.validee replaces by Cursus absence id; status received / unresolved / ignored / error). Dispatch (src/server/integrations/cursus/events.ts + inbox.ts): heures.validees stored, never applied (the timesheet link awaits the owner); absence.validee → the R-031 writer when the matricule resolves (persons.matricule, else cursus_clearances.matricule → person_id), else 'unresolved' kept for replay; verdict.modifie → in HTTP mode find the project through site_sheets.cursus_chantier_id and call refresh() (export it from connector.ts, bypassing the one-minute throttle, keeping in-flight dedup), else 'unresolved' (mock: recorded only); demande.decision stored only; unknown types 'ignored'. One audit row per NEW accepted event (actor 'Cursus', area 'Integrations'), none for duplicates; log / logError. The Cursus row of Activity & integrations shows the last event received and the unresolved count (integrations.ts, Cursus row only). Daily 'cursus-events-purge' job (under your marker) deletes inbox rows older than 13 months. docs/integrations/cursus-contract.md for the Cursus team (path, header, envelope, idempotency and replacement keys, status codes, 429/503 retryable, directory endpoint of R-027), marked as a proposal like TODO(codex). Tests: validation, duplicate, week replacement, route handler (bad key, Entra mode without session, disabled connector → 503, size), the purge job; one e2e post in tests/e2e/d8-connectors.spec.ts; add the route to the R-016 route matrix.

R-031 absences from Cursus, Lucca unplugged (NEW-CURSUS-4 a, NEW-CURSUS-7 a, R-1 a). Writer in src/server/dal/hr.ts (leave functions only; the roles-rates-hr lot edits the rates/hours/skills functions of the same files): provisional, isolated parser of absence.validee { matricule, start, end, nature, id? } reducing nature to 'leave' or 'absence' (nature never stored); replaces rows by external_id (Cursus id, else matricule + start + end), source 'cursus', one row per week covered, days = the week's working days within the range minus the Settings public holidays (listPublicHolidays); unresolved matricules never written to leave_entries. Two types: codes.ts leave codecs, leave_types in both seeds (seed/resources.ts and the production seed/reference.ts, leave functions only), a custom data migration mapping CP/RTT → leave and Sick/Training → absence plus source default 'cursus', legend and cell tone (absence takes the former 'sick' look); getLeaveCalendar adds up several rows for the same person and week (today entries.find keeps the first). Leave screen: remove Sync Lucca (button in hr/primary-buttons.tsx, action in hr/actions.ts, form and parser in hr/forms.ts, their tests, citing NEW-CURSUS-7); the 06:30 tile becomes 'Cursus · last absence received {date}' or 'None received yet' (latest received entry or the inbox); subtitle and hint say Cursus. Lucca → Cursus in leave texts only: src/server/dal/shell.ts:336 STATIC_EXTRA leave (only that line), src/domain/admin.ts:43 holidays sub (only that line), schema/code comments. Leave alone: hours texts (R-030), the Skills 'Certifications' tile (roles-rates-hr), the site collision subtitle (site-quality), the seeded n10 notification and 'Imported 312 h' activity. Tests citing NEW-CURSUS-4/7: domain/hr.test.ts, dal/delivery2.test.ts (LEAVE_DAYS), forms.test.ts (AF.leave), actions.test.ts, hr.render.test.ts. Load, what-if and capacity stay for R-084; collisions are computed by R-119.

R-173 GDPR, buildable part. Leave display and export minimised to the two simple types (follows R-031): drop the 'sick' tone and the « Maladie / arrêt maladie » strings, KPI sub 'Today · leave, RTT, sick, training' reworded without reasons, page comment in resources/leave/page.tsx, leave export cells 'Leave/Absence · n d' and the 'Leave types' sheet collapsed (src/server/export/providers/resources.ts — leave workbook only); days and hours unchanged (85 %, 43 d); hr.test.ts and hr.render.test.ts with NEW-CURSUS-4 comments. No retention-purge job (DPO). R-173 stays en_cours.

### Item scopes (triage, after the adversarial check)

#### R-023 — Connect Vigie's real API (NCR, HSE, ECO) and switch to the Vigie number (partly buildable, S)

Buildable now:
(1) Stop losing undelivered progress on a full refresh. In upsertMirror (vigie/mirror.ts:55-59) and upsertNcrMirror (quality-mirror.ts:42-46), when prune is set, delete a gone row only if it has no outbox entry whose syncStatus is not 'sent'. Put the condition in the DELETE itself (WHERE id IN gone AND NOT EXISTS an undelivered vigie_eco_progress / vigie_ncr_progress row), so a concurrent recordProgress on PostgreSQL cannot slip in between check and delete. The row is pruned at the first refresh after all its entries are sent. No migration is needed. Keep the eco_id / ncr_id 'set null' variant only if the sent history must also survive (it feeds ncrVerificationDecidedCount); that variant needs a new migration and readers that tolerate orphan entries. Tests in delivery4-writes.test.ts and delivery5-writes.test.ts:
- delivery fails and the refresh no longer lists the ECO or NCR: the entry survives and is redelivered by redeliverEcoOutbox / redeliverNcrOutbox;
- an empty refresh keeps rows that have undelivered entries and prunes the others;
- the existing assertion at delivery5-writes.test.ts:123 still holds.
(2) Keystone side of V-3a: in src/features/quality/actions.ts:43, loosen NCR_NUMBER (used at :87 and :116) to a safe generic reference pattern that accepts both NCR-029 and Vigie's own event number (e.g. EV-2026-130), with a test. Optionally update the "NCR-029 (unique per project)" comment on VigieNcrRecord.number in types.ts:156 to say it is Vigie's number.
(3) A draft Keystone ↔ Vigie interface contract (e.g. docs/integrations/vigie-contract.md), built from the existing types and endpoints, covering:
- GET /api/ecos | /api/ncrs | /api/hse ?source=keystone, POST /api/ecos/:number/progress and /api/ncrs/:project/:number/progress;
- the VigieEcoRecord, VigieNcrRecord and VigieHseRecord fields;
- number = Vigie's number (V-3a), vigieRef = Vigie id;
- keystoneId idempotency, the { id } answer, the X-Api-Key header;
- the rule that closed records stay in the lists.
The project-key section must be explicitly marked as waiting for NEW-SYS-1 / R-022. The /signaler parameters are only referenced (they belong to R-025), and the ECO routes are only referenced (they belong to R-024).
Not buildable now: the real endpoints and payload alignment, the Vigie ↔ Keystone project mapping (R-022 / NEW-SYS-1), removal of the TODO(vigie) markers, and end-to-end checks.

Risks: The proposal keeps only rows with undelivered entries: sent entries still cascade-delete with their row. That loses the history behind ncrVerificationDecidedCount (src/server/dal/quality.ts:113) and the Activity & integrations counts (dal/integrations.ts:73-74) whenever Vigie stops listing a record (e.g. if its list drops closed NCRs). · A row kept because Vigie no longer reports it (deleted in Vigie, or moved out of source=keystone) is shown with Vigie's last state as if live. record() (eco-connector.ts:25, quality-connector.ts:30) still accepts new progress on it, which can then never be delivered: a likely 404 every 5 minutes with no retry limit. The retry limit is itself an open platform-web question in DELIVERY-7 §8. Consider refusing new progress on such rows, or marking them. · Race on PostgreSQL: if the undelivered check is a separate SELECT before the DELETE, a progress entry recorded concurrently on a row being pruned is still cascade-deleted. Put the condition in the DELETE (NOT EXISTS) to narrow the window. PGlite (single connection) is not affected. · The contract's project key (projectCode in the payloads and in the NCR progress URL) depends on NEW-SYS-1 / R-022, still open and 'Bloquant production'. A contract handed to Vigie before that decision may have to be reissued. Its /signaler and ECO-route sections duplicate R-025 and R-024. · V-3a is not purely a payload matter. Besides the NCR_NUMBER check, src/features/eco/actions.ts:30 checks ECO_NUMBER = /^ECO-\d{4}-\d{3,}$/. If Vigie's future ECO numbering differs, ECO approvals and steps would be refused the same way. Vigie has no ECO module yet (R-024), so leave it, but it is a coupling to the prototype format. · In demo mode with VIGIE_API_URL set, prototype-seeded NCR-0xx rows that have undelivered entries would never be pruned once Vigie returns its own numbers. Production seeds no Vigie mirror (db/seed/index.ts:139), so this only affects demo or test databases.

#### R-024 — Have Vigie build its ECO module with the proposed addresses (partly buildable, S)

Buildable now (S, documentation and comments only, no behaviour change):

(1) Write the ECO section of the Vigie interface contract, in the same document R-023 uses (proposed: docs/integrations/vigie-contract.md, a new folder). It covers:
- the agreed routes (NEW-D4-1 "a"): /modifications-techniques, /modifications-techniques/<ECO number>, and /modifications-techniques/nouvelle with `part`, `project`, `title`, `source=keystone`; the register filters `part` and `project`. Note that Vigie's other routes use French parameter names; these stay English as agreed.
- the list endpoint proposal: GET /api/ecos?source=keystone returning VigieEcoRecord[] (fields as in types.ts:9-47). It names the statuses (draft, impact_analysis, in_approval, released, rejected), the approval keys (englead, quality, pm; the director holds none), and the decisions and dispositions as in codes.ts.
- the progress endpoint proposal: POST /api/ecos/:number/progress with the body from eco-connector.ts:111-114, acknowledged by `{ id }`.
- refresh at most once a minute, with the full-refresh semantics.

Marked placeholders:
- authentication (V-3, provisional X-Api-Key, shared with R-023);
- the project identifier (NEW-SYS-1 / R-022; the Keystone code for now);
- what happens to progress on an ECO that left approval or that Vigie no longer lists (R-023);
- an OPTIONAL, not agreed, claim-number prefill parameter (notes-quality-site §5.1).

(2) In src/server/integrations/vigie/links.ts, replace the 5 "OPEN QUESTION V-1" markers with a note that the routes and parameters were agreed by NEW-D4-1 "a" (Vigie adopts them). Rename the describe title at src/server/dal/delivery4-writes.test.ts:304. No URL or test assertion changes.

(3) Update reste-a-developper.json: R-024 becomes "en_cours" with a note that the contract has been sent and Vigie's development is awaited, never "fait". Add a history line and run `npm run remaining:page`.

Not buildable now: Vigie's ECO module itself, the real http start (VIGIE_API_URL against real endpoints), the end-to-end check, and aligning the payload with whatever Vigie publishes. The demo ECOs need no work: the production seed has no Vigie mirror, and they stay in the demo seed only.

Risks: The contract document does not exist yet (no docs/integrations/). If R-023 is not done in the same lot, R-024 has to create the document, so the two need to be coordinated to avoid two diverging Vigie contracts. · DELIVERY-7.md §8 still lists V-1 as open, and notes-quality-site §5.1 reuses V-1 for the claim-number extension. Removing the markers must not suggest that the claim-number parameter is agreed; keep it as an explicit open proposal. · The `project` parameter and VigieEcoRecord.projects carry Keystone project codes, but the shared code is undecided (NEW-SYS-1 'Autre réponse', R-022 blocked). The contract must flag this, or Vigie may build its filter on the wrong identifier. · The progress payload identifies the actor only by display name and persona role (features/eco/actions.ts:57,83). With Entra ID sign-in (R-038) Vigie may need a stable user id or e-mail to check approval-key ownership. The contract should propose this rather than freeze the current body, otherwise the payload will need a later change. · Authentication (V-3) is still open. The X-Api-Key header is provisional, so the contract must label it as a proposal. · R-024 is 'bloquant_production' and its core is Vigie's development. Marking it 'fait' after the documentation step would break the rule to mark an item 'fait' only once it is integrated and verified; it must stay 'en_cours' or 'a_faire'. · Delivery 7 reused vigieNewEcoUrl in the claim drawer with the claim number inside `title`. Vigie must accept long, free-text titles in the prefill; the contract should state this.

#### R-025 — Prefill Vigie reports and filter its lists by project (partly buildable, S)

Keystone side only; the end-to-end check stays blocked on Vigie and on NEW-SYS-1 / R-022.

In src/server/integrations/vigie/links.ts:
- Give vigieNcrListUrl, vigieHseListUrl and vigieCoactivityUrl an optional { projectCode } argument, sent as `projet` (French, like the /signaler proposals). Results: /evenements?type=NC&projet=<code>, /prevention?projet=<code>, /prevention/carte?projet=<code>.
