- Reword the V-3 comment (links.ts:66 and the section note) instead of deleting it: the value is the Keystone code until NEW-SYS-1 / R-022 settle the shared project id.

Call sites and tests:
- Pass `code` at src/features/site/model.ts:204.
- vigieNcrListUrl and vigieCoactivityUrl have no screen caller, so they only change signature. Their re-exports in src/server/dal/quality.ts and src/server/dal/site.ts stay as they are.
- Update the test at src/features/site/render.test.ts:118. Add link assertions in src/server/dal/delivery5-writes.test.ts:95-100.

Do not add any new button, such as a "Manage in Vigie" on the NCR register or a co-activity link. In docs/reste-a-developper.json, set R-025 to en_cours, not fait, until Vigie reads the parameters.

Risks: The project value is a placeholder. NEW-SYS-1 is answered 'Autre réponse' (#000 engineering, XXX000 analytic, reconcile on Keystone's project sheet) and R-022 is blocked. If Vigie ends up filtering on its own ids (K-2025-042), every caller must pass a mapped id from the project record, not just the code. The claimed 'one-file change in links.ts' only holds if the mapping is resolved inside links.ts or in a single helper. · The claim that Vigie ignores unknown parameters cannot be checked: the Vigie repository is not on disk. It rests on the routes documented in links.ts:41-43, which list no `projet` on /evenements or /prevention. If Vigie already filters on `projet` with its own ids, sending HLT-026 would make 'Manage in Vigie' open an empty list. · Parameter naming is inconsistent: vigieEcoListUrl / vigieNewEcoUrl use English `project` (a V-1 proposal), while the NCR and HSE links would use French `projet`. Vigie must be told which names are proposals (V-1 / V-3, DELIVERY-7 §8 quality-site). · The visible effect is nil until Vigie evolves. The item must not be marked 'fait' in reste-a-developper.json; only the Keystone half is done. · Deleting the 'OPEN QUESTION V-3' wording would hide the still-open question of which project id Vigie uses (DELIVERY-5 §8 V-3, NEW-SYS-1).

#### R-096 — Rejected receipt: prefilled Vigie supplier NCR and buyer alert (buildable, S)

The scope is buildable as proposed, with these small corrections.

(1) links.ts
- Extend vigieReportNcrUrl with supplier, supplierCode, order, receipt and quantity. They become fournisseur, code_fournisseur, commande, reception and quantite, next to type=NC, projet, article, titre and source=keystone.
- Document them in the comment as a V-3 proposal (SC-3b, NEW-D4-3). Empty values are dropped by the existing qs().
- The current link tests are in src/server/dal/delivery5-writes.test.ts:98, which is not in the file list. Put the new link tests there, or in a new src/server/integrations/vigie/links.test.ts.

(2) Read model
- ReceiptRow has no supplier X3 code. Add suppliers.x3Code to loadReceipts (src/server/dal/deliveries.ts).
- The code is probably empty today (NEW-D4-4: the Sage supplier file is not loaded), so code_fournisseur is usually left out.

(3) Drawer (model.ts, deliveries-view.tsx)
- For a Rejected or Partially rejected receipt, under "Quality check", add one "Declare the supplier NCR in Vigie" link per line with a rejected quantity. With several such lines, suffix the label with "· <ref> × <qty>".
- Use VIGIE_LINK_ATTRS. The link carries the project, part ref, supplier, PO, receipt number, quantity and title.
- Internal viewers only. Nothing is recorded in Keystone.

(4) Buyer notification
- Send it from decide() in actions.ts after the DAL transaction commits, best effort (try/catch plus logError). It covers row Reject, drawer Reject, Partial rejection and the My approvals inbox.
- Prefer a small DAL reader (purchase-order buyer, rejected total) over changing decideReceipt's return type, which acceptReceipt, rejectReceipt, recordReceiptQc and their tests rely on.
- Buyer resolution: submitted_by, else created_by, else the owner of the RFQ that drafted the order, keeping only persons with an active user account.
- The actor is excluded at every step. If the resolved buyer is the actor, nobody is notified: do not fall back.
- If no buyer has an active account, notify the procurement-role users through toPersonIds with the actor removed. Do not use audience roles alone: createNotification with an audience notifies every user of the role, the actor included.
- Notification fields: kind 'delivery', title "Delivery rejected at receipt", sub "BL-… · PO-… · <supplier> · <n> rejected", screen 'deliveries', params { receipt }, projectCode.

(5) Toast
- On a rejection, the toast adds the "declare the supplier NCR in Vigie" segment. It goes through messageText (FR rule or per-segment dictionary).
- The inbox path is English and shown with showEn; keep it consistent there.

(6) i18n FR strings (src/i18n/extra/deliveries.json, plus a RULES entry in src/features/deliveries/i18n.ts for "<n> rejected" and the labels with a suffix).

(7) Tests:
- links: new parameters, and empty values dropped.
- model: links only on rejected lines; none for Accepted, Announced or QC pending.
- actions: buyer resolution order and fallback; actor excluded, including the case where the buyer is the actor; the inbox path notifies; a notification failure leaves the QC committed.
- Render test of the drawer link.

Keep as stated:
- No echo of the NC number on the receipt (a V-3 question).
- The link stays while the receipt is rejected.

Risks: The Vigie parameter names (fournisseur, code_fournisseur, commande, reception, quantite) are Keystone's proposal (V-3 is open; V-3b only lists projet, article, activité, titre, lieu). Today Vigie's /signaler reads only `lieu`, so the prefill does nothing until Vigie adopts the names. If Vigie names them differently, only links.ts changes. This is the same accepted risk as R-112. · Demo seed: every seeded PO has Fabien Bertrand (SEED_BUYER), who is also the procurement persona, as creator and submitter. A rejection decided as procurement therefore notifies nobody, by design (never the actor). Demos and scenario tests must use the quality persona (Hélène Sauvage) as the actor to see the alert. · Fallback to the procurement role: createNotification with only `audience` sends to every user holding the role, the actor included. The fallback must pass toPersonIds of the procurement users minus the actor, or a procurement decider alerts themself. · 'Buyer' = the person who submitted the PO, else its author, else the RFQ owner, is a Keystone default. The owner never defined 'acheteur'. Orders that will come from Sage X3 (I-3b extracts) may carry no Keystone person and will always fall back to the procurement role. · Changing decideReceipt's return type (ReceiptStatus) would ripple through acceptReceipt, rejectReceipt, recordReceiptQc and existing tests. A separate post-commit reader avoids that, but it adds a read after the transaction (stale only in a harmless way). · The supplier X3 code is probably empty everywhere until the Sage supplier file (NEW-D4-4) is imported, so code_fournisseur is almost always left out. · The projet parameter carries the Keystone code. Vigie's own project ids differ ('K-2025-042'; NEW-SYS-1 and V-3 are open), so Vigie may not match the project. This is shared with every existing Vigie link. · Who sees the link is a design choice. The scope gives it to every internal viewer of Deliveries, while SC-3b says 'le contrôleur valide la NC'. Showing it only to QC deciders (canQc) is a defensible alternative; Vigie enforces its own rights either way. · The toast suffix in the My approvals inbox (English plus showEn) points to an action the inbox has no link for. Keep the wording neutral or limit the suffix to the Deliveries screen.

#### R-015 — Notifier les événements clés aux personnes concernées (X-6a) (partly buildable, L)

Bell notifications in Keystone only (X-6a), through one recipients helper built on createNotification with toPersonIds. The helper maps persons to their active user accounts, never notifies the actor, and leaves client users to the existing read-time filter (dal/shell.ts:389-405). Every notice is sent after the writer's transaction commits, from the Server Function or job. A failure is logged with logError and never undoes the write.

Live now:
(1) CR submitted (features/changes/actions.ts after submitChangeRequest). Either build R-069 (sequential chain P-6b) first and notify the approver of the first pending step, then the next step's approver after each decision, or, without R-069, notify the approver of every pending step at submit.
- PM step: the PM of record (default from notes-rights §6 Q4).
- Director step: users with the director role.
- Client step: client users of the project's client organisation (none at launch).
- CCB step: the director (P-6a), not a Settings value.
- On final approval or rejection: the requester (requestedByPersonId).
(2) Delivery refused: after recordReceiptQc or rejectReceipt with rejected units > 0, notify the project leads and the PO's creator or submitter, else the procurement users.
(3) Invoice blocked: invoices of the receipt or its PO whose matchedInvoiceStatus turns from 'awaiting_receipt' to 'blocked' after QC (compare before and after). Notify the PO creator or submitter, else the procurement users, plus the director (prototype n8).
(4) @mention (also closes R-149): parse '@First Last' or '@F. Last' in addTaskComment against persons with an active account and notify the person mentioned. Unknown names are ignored.
(5) Task assigned: tasks created from meeting actions (dal/meetings.ts) and activity to-dos (dal/activity-todos.ts) notify the assignee, unless the assignee is the actor. Tasks born from requests already do this.
(6) KPI alert: new job 'kpi-alerts' (hourly) in src/domain/jobs.ts and src/server/jobs/definitions.ts. It runs getPortfolioAlerts('en') with ALERT_RULE_DEFAULTS and the health thresholds in Settings, at the current data date (Friday freeze waits for S-4). A new table, added with a generated migration, records the notified alert keys. A new key notifies once; it can notify again only after it clears and reappears.
- Project alerts go to the project leads and the director.
- Portfolio-wide alerts (over-allocation, approvals waiting) go to the director.
- The first run needs a baseline so it does not flood the bell with every alert already active.

Dormant until Vigie publishes its API (V-3), built as an isolated hook in refreshFromVigie of the HTTP connectors only (diff before and after the upsert, never inside upsertNcrMirror or upsertMirror, which the seed also calls), and covered by tests with injected records:
(7) NCR raised: notify the project leads, plus the quality referent and engineering lead when they are on the project team.
(8) ECO entering in_approval: notify each pending key holder. The pm key goes to the leads of the affected projects (leadProjectCodes); the quality and englead keys go to users holding those roles.

French texts go in src/i18n/extra/d8-notifications.json. Titles are fixed templates; typed text stays in the sub-line.

Tests per event: recipient set, actor excluded, client exclusion, dedup.

Do not mark R-015 'fait' for events (7) and (8) until the Vigie API exists. Update R-149 and R-121 in reste-a-developper.json.

Risks: createNotification opens its own db.transaction (dal/notifications.ts:103-118). Called from inside a writer's transaction (recordReceiptQc, the Vigie refresh transaction) it can block on PGlite's single connection. With R-010 transaction retries it can also send the same notice twice. Emit notifications only after commit. · The bell passes titles through t() (components/app/shell/page-header.tsx:77). A title holding typed text (CR title, project name in a KPI alert, task title) would be segment-translated, against R-148. Keep titles as fixed English templates and put typed text in the sub-line (fieldsText). · The KPI alert job: getPortfolioAlerts works at DEFAULT_DATA_DATE = STATUS_ISO (S-4 open) with code-default thresholds ('Alert thresholds in Settings' is open in DELIVERY-7 §8). Alerts change only when the data changes. The first run would notify every alert already active (Harbor Line etc.) unless a baseline is stored first. · In getPortfolioAlerts, directorQueue takes the identity from PERSONA_BY_ROLE.director. Run from the worker in Entra mode, it may not match the real director's approval queue. · CR routing depends on R-069 (sequential chain P-6b), which is not built. Without it, either notify all pending approvers at once (as the prototype n1 does: director and client) or build R-069 first. Routing the PM step to every PM or deputy is an open question (notes-rights §6 Q4). · NCR and ECO notifications are dead code in mock mode, the only mode that works today. Marking R-015 'fait' would overstate what works; give them a progress note that they wait on Vigie (V-3). · NCR recipients beyond the leads depend on project team roles. The demo referents exist only on Harbor Line, and real teams wait for the owner's RACI matrix. Fall back to the project leads. · No buyer field on purchase_orders: createdBy or submittedBy may be null on seeded POs, so the fallback to all procurement users will be common. · The bell already holds the seeded prototype notifications (n1-n10), so live and demo notices will be mixed in demo databases. · The notification panel footer still advertises an e-mail digest and Teams (R-036, X-6b). It stays wrong until the mail relay exists; this item is out of scope for that.

#### R-027 — Import Cursus's staff directory (matricule ↔ person) (partly buildable, M)

Build the Keystone half: dormant by default and switched on by configuration once Cursus provides a directory endpoint.

(1) New migration (npm run db:generate). Add persons.matricule (text, unique, nullable), persons.directory_team (text) and persons.directory_synced_at (timestamp).

(2) Pure function in src/domain/directory.ts:
- Match a person by matricule first, then by lower-case work e-mail. Only persons with clientId null can match, so external contacts are never touched.
- An e-mail held by more than one person matches nobody; the entry is reported.
- An entry that matches nobody becomes a new person.
- A new person gets a unique shortName derived from the name ("J. Wagner", with a suffix on collision) and initials.
- An existing person's shortName is never renamed, because records cite people by that text.
- The team maps to a department only when the names are exactly equal. Otherwise it is kept as text in directory_team.

(3) DAL writer importStaffDirectory(entries). It upserts persons and writes audit and activity, never deletes, and never creates users or roles (Entra gives them at sign-in).

(4) Cursus connector: listDirectory() in types.ts and connector.ts, calling GET /api/integrations/keystone/annuaire with X-Api-Key and returning [{ matricule, nom, prenom, email, equipe }]. Mark it TODO(cursus) with a new open question. The mock returns nothing ("not connected").

(5) A separate switch in src/server/config/parse.ts and .env.example (for example CURSUS_DIRECTORY=on). Without it, a CURSUS_API_URL Cursus that lacks the endpoint would answer 404 every day.

(6) A daily job 'cursus-directory' in src/domain/jobs.ts and src/server/jobs/definitions.ts that respects connectorEnabled. Also run the import from the existing "Sync now" of the Cursus row (src/server/dal/integrations.ts:184).

(7) Use the mapping:
- recordsOfPayload and the refresh take names from persons.matricule before the mirror's own names (connector.ts:133-163).
- upsertClearances sets personId by matricule (mirror.ts:19-23).
- Change the rule at mirror.ts:22 that keeps the old personName when the matricule matches. Otherwise a row first named after the matricule ("EMP-0018") keeps that name forever.
- cursus_requests already copy the clearance row's matricule (connector.ts:57), so no change is needed there.

(8) Tests: the pure matching function, plus the import and the job with a stubbed fetch.

Risks: The endpoint and payload are guesses: Cursus has no directory access and the answer says Cursus needs a new access. Cursus may choose different field names, team codes instead of names, or paging, so expect rework when it ships (the same accepted risk as TODO(codex)). · Step (7) does nothing for now, even in http mode: cursus_chantier_id is never written (only seed/site.ts:28 sets it, to null; R-028 is blocked), so no real go-no-go refresh runs for any site. · The production sign-in gap is not closed: with the production seed nobody can sign in until a real directory or a first list loads persons with e-mails (DELIVERY-7 §8 'auth', notes-auth.md:141-144). This item does not answer that question. · cursus_clearances is unique on (project_code, person_name), and reminders are linked to clearances by personName (mirror.ts:57, connector.ts:46-51). Renaming a clearance row from its matricule to the directory name can detach existing reminders and outbox rows, or hit the unique constraint. · persons.email is not unique, and sign-in refuses an ambiguous e-mail (users.ts:126-129, domain/auth.ts:157). The import must not create a second person with an existing e-mail, and must not overwrite the e-mail of an external contact. · Demo data: prototype persons are mostly known by name only, without e-mail or matricule. A real directory imported on demo data would create duplicates, such as 'J. Wagner (2)'. Keep the import off by default and use it with the production seed. · Department scoping: users.departmentId is copied from the person at each sign-in. Teams that do not match a department leave departmentId null, which can change department-head views. Teams that do not match are logged, never invented. · Adding a job changes JOB_SCHEDULES and JOB_NAMES. jobs.test.ts and the job_locks expectations must be updated.

#### R-029 — Créer le point d'entrée des événements poussés par Cursus (buildable, M)

1. POST /api/integrations/cursus/events, a Route Handler with no viewer and no same-origin check. The X-Api-Key header is compared in constant time. The path is added to isPublicPath, with tests in src/domain/auth and src/proxy.test.ts. Body limit 256 KB. New 'events' rate-limit bucket keyed by client address, with a generous limit: Cursus re-sends its backlog after an outage. Returns 503 + Retry-After when connectorEnabled('Cursus') is false or the database is unavailable.
2. A pure zod envelope { id, type, emis_le, data } in src/domain/cursus-events.ts, with unit tests. It accepts the known heures.validees shape { matricule, semaine, projets: { code: heures } } and an optional lignes[] (a proposal for NEW-CURSUS-5 c).
3. A cursus_events inbox table, via a new migration from npm run db:generate. The event id is unique; a duplicate answers 200 { duplicate: true }. heures.validees for the same (matricule, semaine) replaces the previous event in one transaction. absence.validee replaces by Cursus absence id. Status received / unresolved / ignored / error.
4. One audit row per reception: actor 'Cursus', area 'Integrations', following the IT_FILE_ACTOR precedent. Log through log/logError.
5. Dispatch by type, limited to what can work today:
   - heures.validees: stored, not applied (R-030 waits for the owner).
   - absence.validee: stored. Marked 'unresolved' when the matricule matches no person, which is every case until R-027.
   - verdict.modifie: in HTTP mode, find the project through site_sheets.cursus_chantier_id and call refresh() directly (exported, bypassing the one-minute throttle). Otherwise 'unresolved', and in mock mode recorded only.
   - demande.decision: stored only. No new cursus_requests columns until travel requests have a caller and Cursus's payload is known.
   - Unknown types: 'ignored'.
6. Status codes: 200 / 400 / 401 / 413 / 429 / 503.
7. The Cursus row of Activity & integrations shows the last event received and the count of unresolved events.
8. The contract goes in the lot notes for the Cursus team (path, header, envelope, idempotency and replacement keys, status codes, retry rules), marked as a proposal like TODO(codex).
9. CURSUS_EVENTS_KEY in parse.ts and .env.example. In production the endpoint refuses every call when no key is configured, and refuses the demo key.
10. Tests: unit tests (validation, duplicate, week replacement), Route Handler tests (bad key, Entra mode without a session, disabled connector → 503), one e2e post. Add the route to the R-016 route matrix.
Not in scope: applying hours (R-030), applying absences to leave and load (R-031), retroactively re-applying unresolved events (R-027 / R-022 / R-028).

Risks: The owner may still consider the 'Cursus timesheet link' unvalidated. CLAUDE.md and DELIVERY-7.md §6, both written after the 2026-10-09 answers, list it as not built by decision. Any heures.validees handling beyond raw storage (imputation, the Heures screen, removing hour entry) would exceed the validation. · The envelope { id, type, emis_le, data } and the event id used for idempotency are Keystone's proposal. Cursus's real event-log format cannot be checked here: the sibling repo is not in the session and must not be modified. The contract may need rework when Cursus switches from its log to HTTP POST. · In practice almost nothing will resolve. persons has no matricule (R-027 blocked), site_sheets.cursus_chantier_id is never written (R-028 blocked) and travel requests are never created (requestTravel has no caller). The endpoint mostly accumulates 'unresolved' events until those items land, so re-processing must be designed in. · Key handling: the outbound loopback exception in parse.ts:214-224 is keyed on CURSUS_API_URL and does not carry over to inbound calls. Accepting the 'demo-keystone' key from any caller whenever Cursus is on loopback would open the endpoint on a network-reachable production host. clientAddress relies on X-Forwarded-For, which can be forged without a TLS proxy. · Using httpCursusConnector.getGoNoGo for verdict.modifie would be swallowed by the one-minute refresh throttle (REFRESH_EVERY_MS). refresh() must be exported and called directly, keeping the in-flight dedup. · One audit row per reception, including duplicates and replays, can bloat audit_entries during Cursus re-send storms. The 13-month inbox purge is a new worker job that needs its own interval and test. · Real traffic depends on things outside Keystone: hosting (I-1a) for Cursus to reach Keystone, and Cursus switching its event log to POST. End-to-end value cannot be verified with real Cursus in delivery 8; tests only. · The rate limit is per process and in memory (R-003). A 429 during a backlog re-send must be documented as retryable in the contract.

#### R-031 — Recevoir les absences de Cursus et débrancher Lucca (partly buildable, M)

1) Absence intake, kept in src/server/integrations/cursus/ plus a DAL writer in src/server/dal/hr.ts. The parser of absence.validee is marked provisional: { matricule, start, end, nature, optional Cursus id }. Keystone reduces nature to the type 'leave' or 'absence' and never stores it. The writer replaces the rows by external_id, source 'cursus', one row per week covered. Days = the week's working days within the range minus the public holidays of Settings (listPublicHolidays). The replace key is the Cursus id when present, otherwise matricule + start + end. The R-029 dispatch calls the writer if R-029 is built in the same delivery. Otherwise the writer is tested with fixtures and a mock. Matricules resolve through the existing Cursus mirror (cursus_clearances.matricule → person_id), and later through R-027. Unresolved ones are kept for replay and never written to leave_entries. 2) Two types, Leave and Absence (NEW-CURSUS-4): codes.ts, the leave_types seed in both seeds, a hand-written data migration with the next free number (precedent 0012_*_backfill) mapping CP/RTT → leave and Sick/Training → absence, the source default 'cursus', the legend, the cell tone (absence takes the former 'sick' look), and golden or DAL tests citing NEW-CURSUS-4 (domain/hr.test.ts, dal/delivery2.test.ts compare with LEAVE_DAYS). getLeaveCalendar must add up several rows for the same person and week; today `entries.find` keeps only the first. 3) Leave screen: remove the Sync Lucca button, action, form, parser and their tests (forms.test.ts compares with AF.leave, so cite NEW-CURSUS-7). The 06:30 tile becomes 'Cursus · last absence received {date}' or 'None received yet', read from the latest received entry (or from R-029's inbox if it exists). Update the subtitle and hint to say Cursus. 4) Replace Lucca with Cursus in the leave and clearance texts: shell.ts:336, site-view.tsx:316, domain/admin.ts:43, skills-view.tsx:37, schema and code comments, FR/EN entries. The hours-related texts stay for R-030: screens.ts:73, home.ts:636, steering export, hr i18n hours lines, rates-view, and the seeded n10 notification and 'Lucca' activity, which describe an hours import. Reword those only as leave-only text, or leave them for R-030. 5) Leave collisions are not in this item: move them to R-119 (D-6b, automatic update plus manual arbitration). R-031 may only add a pure domain helper `absenceOverlapsRotation` for R-119 to use. 6) Resources load, what-if and prioritisation capacity stay with R-084.

Risks: The absence.validee payload is not documented in Keystone (only 'matricule, dates, nature'), and the Cursus repository is not available to check. Field names, the nature codes and whether an id exists are guesses. The parser must be isolated and agreed with the Cursus team. · Without a Cursus id, a corrected absence with new dates cannot replace the old one, and a cancelled absence never disappears. Stale absences would lower availability until an 'absence.annulee' or empty-range rule is agreed. · R-029 (endpoint, API key, inbox) is not started and waits on an undefined contract. If it is not built in the same delivery, the handler and the 'last absence received' KPI have no caller or data source. · R-027 is blocked on Cursus's directory. persons has no matricule and leave_entries.person_id is NOT NULL, so in production every received absence stays unresolved, and the matricules seeded in cursus_clearances are null. A replay path is needed once matricules resolve. · The Leave grid's weeks come from the demo allocations (fixed 28 Sep – 16 Nov 2026). The production seed has no allocations or pool people, so received absences stay invisible there until R-085/R-013 (S-4). · leave_entries.days is an integer and the payload carries dates only: half-day leave, which is common in French HR, cannot be represented without a numeric column. · Counting computed leave collisions in Go (src/domain/site.ts:104) without R-119's arbitration adds a NO GO that nobody can clear. Rotations have no writer and the seed overlaps make three new HLT-026 collisions, which also changes proto-golden-site. · Mapping Training to 'absence' and dropping Sick as a reason changes displayed demo values: tests in domain/hr.test.ts, dal/delivery2.test.ts, forms.test.ts, actions.test.ts and hr.render.test.ts must cite NEW-CURSUS-4/NEW-CURSUS-7. · Relabelling the n10 notification or the 'Imported 312 h' activity to Cursus would suggest the Cursus timesheet link, which is still awaiting validation (BACKLOG, R-030). · 'Tracked with expiry in Cursus' on Skills would describe certifications as Cursus data, while they are a seed-only Keystone table with no Cursus feed.

#### R-173 — Mettre Keystone en conformité RGPD (données personnelles, conservation, sous-traitants) (partly buildable, S)

(1) NEW-CURSUS-4 minimisation, buildable now:
- Display and export absences as a simple type only: Leave for CP and RTT, Absence for sick, training and anything else.
- Drop the 'sick' tone (domain/hr.ts LeaveLabel, LeaveCellTone, leaveCell; leave-view.tsx CELL map and legend).
- Remove « Maladie / arrêt maladie » (features/hr/i18n.ts, fr-dict.json entries).
- Replace the KPI sub « Today · leave, RTT, sick, training » with a wording that has no reason, and fix the page comment in app/(app)/resources/leave/page.tsx.
- In the leave export (export/providers/resources.ts), export cells as Leave/Absence · n d, and collapse or remove the « Leave types » sheet so it no longer lists the sick code.
- Keep the days and hours, so capacity and KPIs are unchanged (85 %, 43 d).
- Keep the internal codes in the demo seed. Optionally reduce the production seed's reference leave_types to the two simple types.
- Update src/domain/hr.test.ts (the 'Sick'/'sick' cell) and src/features/hr/hr.render.test.ts (the KPI sub, 'Sick 21 h', the legend) with comments citing NEW-CURSUS-4.
- Lucca wording stays for R-031.

(3) Add a configuration warning in src/server/config/parse.ts, logged at start-up by the existing warnings loop, when ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is set (AI enabled) in production or with KEYSTONE_SEED=production. It reminds that transcripts go to Anthropic and that the sub-processing agreement must be signed. No behaviour change. Document it in .env.example.

(2) Optional only: a 'retention-purge' job in the R-006 pattern for notifications (recipients cascade), activity_events and export_log, with a duration per category from configuration, unset by default, so it is a no-op. Exclude orphan files (already pruned by 'orphan-files-prune') and audit_entries (append-only ISO 9001 trail; retention or pseudonymisation is the DPO's and quality's call).

Not buildable now:
- who may see whose absences on Leave;
- the actual retention durations;
- the register of processing, legal basis, and the host and Anthropic DPAs;
- rights of access and erasure;
- storing only dates and type from Cursus (R-031, blocked on absence.validee events).

Risks: The owner's NEW-CURSUS-4 detail describes what Keystone keeps from Cursus. Applying it now to the Lucca demo mirror anticipates R-031. The Lucca labels ('Lucca sync', 'Approved in Lucca') stay until R-031, so the screen will be minimised but still named after Lucca. · Mapping Training to 'Absence' (and RTT to 'Leave') is a default the owner did not spell out. It should be listed as a default to confirm. · If the production seed's leave_types are left as cp/rtt/sick/training, the reason category still exists as reference data in production. Only the display and export are minimised, not storage, while the answer says 'ne garde que'. · The retention-purge placeholder may build the wrong mechanism: the DPO may choose pseudonymisation or different categories. Purging audit_entries would contradict its append-only ISO 9001 role. Purging activity_events or notifications removes history that the feed and the bell show. · A permanent production warning about the Anthropic DPA becomes noise once the DPA is signed, unless it can be acknowledged. Adding an acknowledgement variable would be new configuration to justify. · fr-dict.json and features/hr/i18n.ts carry several 'sick' strings; missing one, or the export's 'Leave types' sheet, leaves the reason visible in FR or in Excel. · Calendar compartmentalisation (who sees whose absences) stays open. Anyone with the leave screen (pm, depthead, member, englead, quality, procurement) still sees everyone's absences, even if only the reason is masked.

### Item scopes (triage, after the adversarial check)

#### R-033 — Construire le connecteur Codex (index, approbations, dépôts, liens profonds) (partly buildable, M)

1) Links module src/server/integrations/codex/links.ts: pure and client-safe, with CODEX_LINK_ATTRS like cursus/links.ts. It provides codexHomeUrl, codexDocumentUrl(number, rev?), codexTypeRegisterUrl() and codexSelectionUrl(numbers), all built on Codex's home with query parameters Codex ignores today, marked TODO(codex) D-1. codexDocumentUrl prefers documents.codexUrl once the index read fills it, so Codex's own link wins over a guess.
- Make OpenCodexButton a link (new tab) for: the document drawer "Open in Codex" (document-drawer.tsx), the vault banner "Open Codex" (vault.tsx), and the register "Edit in Codex" (doc-types.tsx; still hidden outside the workspace).
- My approvals Codex rows already link to Codex's home. Only switch their href to codexDocumentUrl (src/domain/approvals.ts codexVaultItems, src/server/approvals/codex-documents.ts).
- "Download zip" keeps its explanatory toast ("needs Codex — the files are stored in Codex"). Only its link becomes codexSelectionUrl(selection). It is not converted into a bare link until Codex serves files (D-2 context).

2) Honest connector state, with no dependency.
- connectorLink returns 'live' for Codex when the Codex mode is 'http' (src/domain/connectors.ts, plus its test).
- The Codex row shows pending and failed counts from undeliveredCodexEntries (src/server/dal/integrations.ts or admin-connectors.ts).
- "Sync now" on the Integrations screen runs getCodexConnector().sync() before the index refresh (admin-connectors.ts syncAdminConnector; today it never delivers the outbox).
- syncConnector records lastSyncAt and status for a live Codex, not only for kind !== 'static'.
- codexSyncToastT stops claiming "index up to date" in 'http' mode unless the index was actually read: it reports delivered, pending and refreshed (display.ts).
- Update the tests that expect 'Opening Codex · …' toasts and the Codex 'mock' row.

3) HTTP index read, as an isolated TODO(codex) proposal (D-2 / R-127 scope): GET /api/documents?project=…&since=… returns the record, its current revision and its current circuit.
- applyCodexIndex in codex/mirror.ts upserts documents (including codexId and codexUrl), the current document_revisions row and document_approvals.
- It skips, and logs, records whose project or type code Keystone does not know. It never falls back to projectId null (QMS).
- In HTTP mode, syncCodexIndex returns the real count instead of the local count.
- A 'codex-index' job runs hourly in HTTP mode only (domain/jobs.ts, server/jobs/definitions.ts), as an interval default to be confirmed.
- Mock mode is unchanged. In production without Codex the index stays empty (A-15; the production seed already leaves documents empty).
- Tests: links, connector state and counts, mirror upsert including unknown project and type, and a stub Codex over HTTP.

Out of scope (waits for Codex): real routes, the pre-filled deposit form (R-125), file downloads and the zip, and webhooks.

Risks: Deposits and the HTTP index read clash: with CODEX_API_URL set, depositInCodex still creates Keystone-numbered records (count + 40) that Codex never receives. They stay in the index as phantom Drafts next to Codex's real records, which contradicts A-15. Either block the local deposit in HTTP mode (open Codex instead), or accept the gap and document it until R-125. · Project key: the proposed ?project= and the document numbers use Keystone's code (HLT-026). NEW-SYS-1 was answered 'other' and is unresolved: engineering uses #000 codes, finance XXX000. Codex probably does not know Keystone's codes, so the index contract is likely to change. · Unknown references: typeCode is a foreign key to document_types (the register is owned by Codex but seeded locally), and authors and approvers are matched to persons by name. An upsert that does not skip or tolerate unknowns either fails the whole sync, or files a project document as QMS (projectId null), where every project's vault shows it. · The links open Codex's home with parameters Codex ignores, so 'Open in Codex' never shows the document itself. In production, if NEXT_PUBLIC_CODEX_URL is unset, the links point at http://localhost:3100. There is only a startup warning, the same as Cursus and Vigie. · Changing toasts to links breaks existing tests: page.test.ts expects 'Opening Codex · project HLT-026'; actions.test.ts, delivery5.test.ts, model.test.ts and connectors.test.ts expect the Codex row as 'mock' with a simulated sync. Update them, citing the decision ids. · The whole HTTP index path is code that cannot be verified against a real Codex (as with the D7 approvals endpoints). The payload is much larger than the approvals state, so rework is likely once Codex publishes its API (open question D-1, operations Q7 in DELIVERY-7 §8). · Hourly polling departs from the spec's 'Real time (webhooks)' frequency for the Codex row. Job intervals are already an open question (DELIVERY-7 §8, platform-web).

#### R-127 — Limiter la reprise Codex à la fiche, à la révision et au circuit en cours (partly buildable, S)

Build now (D-2 = a, A-15 = a). The per-document Codex route stays a placeholder until D-1 / R-033.

1. Drawer (document-drawer.tsx, model.ts drawerView):
   - « Revisions » shows only the current revision row: note, date · author · working versions, status, Native / PDF.
   - Below it, add a link « Earlier revisions and download log in Codex ». It opens in a new tab and points to `documents.codexUrl` when set, otherwise `codexPublicUrl()` (the Codex home), marked TODO(codex) D-1. A small helper in src/server/integrations/codex/ is fine.
   - Hide this link for the client role.
   - Remove the « Download log » section.
   - Keep: approval proof (current circuit, with the delivery 7 pending-sync rows), Distribution with the client acknowledgement, and the withdrawal callout.
2. DAL (documents.ts):
   - getDocumentRecord returns the current revision only and no downloads.
   - logDocumentDownload stops writing `document_downloads`.
   - Keep the P-10a/b client check in logDownloadAction.
   - Do not leave the buttons silent: give them the same hand-off toast as « Download zip » (the files are in Codex, with an "Open Codex" link) until R-033 serves the files. Recording downloads in the audit trail is optional; the audit trail records no read events today, and it would rebuild a Keystone download log.
3. KPI « Obsolete revisions withdrawn »: do NOT go back to the revision-letter sum, which 00 §7.16 / 10 OQ16 forbid. Either:
   - (a) add a record-level count mirrored from Codex (e.g. a `documents.withdrawn_revision_count` column, TODO(codex) payload field). Seed it with today's withdrawn-row count per document so seeded values stay the same (db-check 25). applyCodexDocumentStates increments it when Codex reports `withdrawnRevision`, replacing the revision-row update at mirror.ts:66-68. vaultKpis reads the column. Or
   - (b) keep the existing revision rows on the server for the KPI only.
4. Seed: current revision row only; no synthetic Codex download log (the demo database may keep this history under A-15, so this step is optional).
5. Migration through `npm run db:generate` (existing migrations untouched): drop `document_downloads`, add the count column if option (a) is chosen. Remove DocDownloadSource from codes.ts.
6. Tests and checks, each with a comment citing D-2:
   - render.test.ts:163-200
   - delivery3.test.ts:86, :113-136
   - overlays.test.ts:49, :64
   - actions.test.ts:144-155
   - domain/documents.test.ts:26
   - scripts/db-check.ts:97-98 table counts
   Add the FR strings in src/i18n/extra. Leave the golden generator scripts unchanged.

Risks: Scope item 4 as proposed (derive the KPI from the current revision letter) brings back a defect listed in docs/spec/00 §7.16 / 10 OQ16. It also miscounts Release-proposed records (the previous revision is not yet withdrawn) and Obsolete records. · The triage file list misses tests that will fail: delivery3.test.ts (revs/dl vs delivery3.golden.json, KPI test name), overlays.test.ts (« Journal des téléchargements » / « Download log »), actions.test.ts:144-155, domain/documents.test.ts:26, and the db-check table counts (document_revisions 68 → 42, document_downloads 60). · Clients open this drawer (the documents page lets them open shared records, and client-view.tsx:294 links to it). The new « … in Codex » link must be hidden for the client role, since clients have no Codex access. · Without the download log, the Download PDF / Native buttons (drawer, vault row, client portal) do nothing visible because Keystone serves no file. They need a Codex hand-off toast, otherwise they become dead buttons. · Writing every download to the audit trail adds the first read event to an audit trail that records only changes, and rebuilds a Keystone download log that D-2 sends to Codex. · Dropping `document_downloads` destroys data. It is harmless before go-live (no production hosting yet, I-1a), but it cannot be undone once a production database exists. · The per-document deep link stays a link to the Codex home until D-1 / R-033. Users must search for the record in Codex to see its history. · Once the real Codex connector exists, the mirror payload (TODO(codex), D-1) must carry the withdrawn-revision count, or the KPI only counts withdrawals seen after connection.

#### R-128 — Rendre cliquables les relations Livrable, Commande et Réserve d’un document (buildable, S)

1. DAL (`src/server/dal/documents.ts`, in `getDocumentRecord` only)
- Resolve each relation of a project record against the record's own project:
  - deliverable → `deliverables.number` in that project;
  - purchase_order → `purchase_orders.number` in that project, falling back to `x3Ref`. SC-4b says Sage X3 edits the order document, so real Codex records may carry the X3 number.
  - punch_item → `punch_items.number`, ITP register first, then site. Keep this lookup in one small function so R-117 (A-6, single list per project) can replace it.
- QMS records, and refs not found in the record's project, give null.
- Each link carries `target: { screen, project, register? } | null`.

2. `src/features/docs/model.ts` `linkHref`
- deliverable → `deliverableHref(project, ref)`;
- purchase_order → `ordersHref(project, { po: ref })`;
- punch_item on the ITP register → `itpHref(project, 'punch') + '#punch-' + ref`;
- punch_item on the site register → `siteHref(project, 'exec', 'punch-' + ref)`;
- article and change_request unchanged.
- Optional: return an inert pill when `canSee(role, screen)` is false. Narrow `DrawerInput.viewer.role` to `RoleKey`. Decide whether Article and CR get the same gating, so the rule is consistent.

3. Row anchors
- Add `id="punch-{number}"` to the ITP `PunchRow` `<tr>` (`src/features/itpcom/itp-view.tsx`) and to the site punch `<tr>` (`src/features/site/site-view.tsx:458-459`). Hash navigation then scrolls to the row.
- Do NOT rely on CSS `:target` for the highlight: Next's `Link` uses `pushState`, which does not update `:target`.
- If a highlight is wanted, read a `?punch=` search param server-side, like `?dlv=` / `?po=` (update the itp and site page URL-state comments), or use a small client component that reads `location.hash`.

4. Update the `DrawerLinkView` comment (`model.ts:181`).

5. Tests (`src/features/docs/render.test.ts`, around lines 211-216)
- PHO-HLT-011 → P-01 resolves to the ITP punch list with anchor `#punch-P-01`.
- PO-2026-118 stays inert (unknown order).
- PID-HLT-026 → D-016 gives `/projects/HLT-026/quality?tab=dlv&dlv=D-016`.
- A record deposited with a real HLT-026 order (for example PO-1082 through the deposit's free-text "Linked purchase order") links to `/projects/HLT-026/orders?po=PO-1082`.
- A QMS record and an unknown ref stay inert. If gating is chosen, a role without the target screen stays inert.
- An ITP / site render test checks the row ids.

Leave the seed's PO-2026-118 / 104 / 109 as they are; whether to remap them to real HLT-026 orders is a question for the owner.

Risks: The proposed CSS `:target` highlight will not fire when the user clicks a relation in the drawer. Next 16.4 App Router navigates with `history.pushState` and only scrolls via `scrollIntoView`, and `pushState` does not update `:target`. Use a `?punch=` param or a client-side hash reader, or drop the highlight. · A-6 was answered « a » (one punch list per project, unique numbers), but R-117 is not done. The 'ITP first, then site' resolution and the two anchor targets are a stopgap that R-117 will rework. Schedule R-128 after R-117, or keep the punch lookup in one place. · Purchase-order relations in Codex will likely carry Sage X3 order numbers (SC-4b: Sage X3 edits the order document), not Keystone's PO-10xx numbers. Without an `x3Ref` fallback the order link may never resolve on real data. A-1a (how purchasing is split between Keystone and X3) is still « À discuter ». · In the seeded demo none of the purchase-order relations resolve (PO-2026-118 / 104 / 109 are not HLT-026 orders), so the order link is invisible in the demo. It needs a deposited fixture in tests, and the owner may expect the demo data remapped. · `canSee` only hides navigation: role-screens.ts says a screen outside the role's list renders read-only with a banner. Gating the new links with `canSee`, while the Article and CR links are not gated, makes the drawer inconsistent. `DrawerInput.viewer.role` is typed as a plain string and needs narrowing to `RoleKey`. · NCR-HLT-029 links to P-01, which matches the site item (site register, NCR-029) at least as well as the ITP item (also about NCR-029, already Closed). 'ITP first' sends that NCR document to the ITP row. · The drawer can open records of other projects (scope=all). The DAL lookup must use the record's own project (as `linkHref` already does with `r.project`), never the page's project, and must add only light queries to `getDocumentRecord`.

#### R-138 — Client portal: document categories computed from the Codex index (L-9) (partly buildable, M)

Build now (L-9), as the triage scoped it, with these corrections.

(1) Domain: add a pure `clientDocCategories(sharedDocs)` in src/domain/rexclient.ts.
- It returns [{key, category (EN source label), fileCount, description (the distinct type labels joined ' · '), latestIssuedOn}] in a fixed order, and leaves out categories with no files.
- One isolated constant, commented L-9, maps type to category: MIN → Meeting minutes, HSE → HSE reports (or "HSE documents", see risks), PV → Accepted deliverables, CTR → Contract & amendments.
- Any other type falls back to its Codex family label (eng / client / purchasing …).
- Keys must be stable for the URL.

(2) DAL client.ts:
- Drop the client_document_categories read and import.
- Compute the categories from the `docs` that listSharedDocuments already returns.
- `documentCategories` becomes {key, category, description, fileCount: number, latestIssuedOn}.

(3) UI:
- model.ts gains a categories view: name through t(), "{n} files", type labels through t().
- client-view.tsx DocumentsTab gets a "By category" strip above the table.
- An optional filter `?tab=docs&cat=<key>` with an "All" chip; an unknown cat falls back to All. This needs parsing in `src/app/(app)/projects/[code]/client/page.tsx`, a `cat` argument on getClientScreen in screen.ts, and a `cat` parameter on clientHref.
- Document titles keep the current exactText / dataText handling (R-148).

(4) Remove the typed data:
- Drop the clientDocumentCategories table in schema/client.ts.
- Generate a new migration (DROP TABLE) with `npm run db:generate`, after 0015; never edit 0004.
- Remove the insert and import in seed/client.ts and update the comment.
- Remove client_document_categories from scripts/db-check.ts:126 and 134.
- Update delivery5.test.ts:413 (6 categories / 7 files) and delivery5-writes.test.ts:459 (table list).

(5) i18n: add only the missing FR strings ("{n} files", the strip title, the filter's aria/labels) in src/i18n/extra. The category labels already exist in fr-dict.json.

(6) Tests:
- domain unit test (HLT-026: 6 categories / 7 files);
- DAL test;
- render test of the strip and of the cat filter;
- screen.test keeps the golden docs list for the default (All).

Not built:
- "Contract & amendments" visible on HLT-026: CTR is client=false in the Codex mirror, and the share flag is Codex's.
- "Monthly progress reports": there is no Codex type for them, and Keystone's status report is not a Codex record. The owner must say whether it should be deposited in Codex.
- Real counts beyond the demo index wait for R-033 (Codex connector). They will follow automatically.

Risks: The HSE type in the Codex mirror is labelled 'HSE plan' (HSE-HLT-002 'Prevention plan'). Filing it under 'HSE reports' mislabels it, and the prototype's 'HSE reports' (monthly safety statistics, incident reports) are Vigie site-HSE records, which never appear in the Codex index. A neutral label such as 'HSE documents', or the family fallback, avoids a misleading category. · The family fallback adds categories that are not among the five prototype ones (Engineering, Client deliverables, Purchasing). 'Client deliverables' next to 'Accepted deliverables' can confuse the client, and MAN-HLT-015, linked to D-015, lands under 'Client deliverables' rather than 'Accepted deliverables'. The owner should confirm the list. Keep it one constant so the owner's list can replace it. · Only the grouping display is asked by L-9. The clickable `?cat=` filter is an extra interaction that neither the prototype nor the answer asks for. It is low risk but should stay minimal (or be a plain grouped display) under the no-new-tools rule. · The file list leaves out page.tsx and the clientHref / getClientScreen signature change. Without them the `cat` URL state cannot reach the view. · The new DROP TABLE migration removes data on any existing database. It is demo data copied from the prototype and is absent from the production seed, but the Delivery 5 backfill test and seedDelivery5 must be updated together, or the backfill fails on a missing table. · Owner expectation: 'Contract & amendments' and 'Monthly progress reports' will not appear on HLT-026. The delivery notes should say why (Codex share flag; no Codex type) so the owner does not read it as a gap. · The scope's return shape is inconsistent (the domain function returns latest issuedOn, but the DAL type omits it). Pick one shape so the tests and the view agree.

Same scope as proposed. In forms.ts, add required number fields 'res' "Resource fit (1–5)" and 'client' "Client priority (1–5)" after 'risk' and before 'comment', with defaultValue '3'. parseScoreProposal validates them with validScore and the same error message, and ScoreProposalInput gains res and client. In prioritisation.ts, ProposalScore gains res and client, and saveProposalScore upserts all 5 axes with the scorer, date and comment. The "score 1 when new" branch stays only as a fallback for an axis added later. The audit text lists the 5 axes, and the JSDoc and file comments are updated. scoreProposalAction passes the 5 values. Add FR entries for the 2 new labels: "Adéquation ressources (1–5)" and "Priorité client (1–5)", in pfinance.json or a new d8 dictionary registered in src/i18n/extra/index.ts. Update the tests:
- actions.test.ts: the AF golden check gets an L-5a comment and expects the prototype fields plus res and client inserted before comment. Update the scoreProposalAction calls, the expected listPortfolioScores arrays, the rank-88 arithmetic, the audit string and the parseScoreProposal expectation, and the saveProposalScore calls at l.113-114.
- delivery3-writes.test.ts:245-248: update the call, the scores and the audit string.
- Add a test that res and client are stored and change the rank.

After merge, follow the standing rule: update docs/reste-a-developper.json (R-134 progress, history), run npm run remaining:page, and republish the page.

Risks: Re-scoring now overwrites the seeded Resource fit and Client priority with whatever is typed, and the dialog cannot prefill a proposal's current scores (FormDialog defaults are static, form-def.ts). A user who leaves the defaults turns EWA-051's resource fit from 2 into 3. This is accepted as the proposed default, but it should be stated in the delivery notes. · Test isolation: the 'restore the seed' call at actions.test.ts:103 must also pass res '2' and client '4' for EWA-051 (seed [4,4,3,2,4]). Otherwise the golden ranking and weights checks run later in the same file can drift. The expected arrays at l.94 and l.101 and the rank-88 arithmetic at l.97-99 all change. · The form-vs-prototype golden check (actions.test.ts:122-131, pfinance.golden.json af.def) has to diverge on purpose. Keep the prototype's 4 fields and comment in order, and cite L-5a, instead of regenerating the golden. · The form's labels still differ from the ranking's axis names: 'Financial return' scores the 'Margin / NPV' axis. L-5a says 'the same axes as the ranking', so the owner might expect matching names. Renaming would break the prototype FR golden labels (af.fr.labels), so keep the prototype labels and record this as a deviation. · Margin at completion (R-132) is computed and shown in its own column, but the Margin / NPV axis score is still entered by hand: L-5a option b was not chosen. Do not tie the two together. · If a new d8-*.json dictionary is created, it must be registered in src/i18n/extra/index.ts, and each key must keep a single French translation across dictionaries (dictionaries.test.ts).

#### R-135 — Projects map: stage-coloured tag, 'Clos / annulé' legend, keyboard markers (buildable, M)

Same scope as the triage, with these corrections.
1) Stage tag colour. In model.ts:87, colour the tooltip stage tag with tagTone(lifecycleTone(p.project.status)), the rule the Sites list uses at l.102. The health tag and markerStyleOf stay as they are. Remove tooltipStatusTone and its import (model.ts:10, worldmap.test.ts:13) or mark it as the prototype's rule. 2) Legend. labels.closed = t('Closed / cancelled'), with FR 'Clos / annulé' in src/i18n/extra/worldmap.json. The fr-dict entry 'Closed / on hold' becomes unused and can stay or go.
3) Keyboard, in projects-map.tsx and projects-map.module.css. The svg takes role='group' and keeps its aria-label. Each cluster <g> gets tabIndex=0 (or -1 when its screen position tr.k*c.x+tr.x and tr.k*c.y+tr.y falls outside 0..W and 0..H), role='button', and aria-label = tooltipTitle (plus the project name for a single marker). Focus shows the hover tooltip; blur hides it unless it is pinned. In onKeyDown, Enter or Space (with preventDefault) applies clusterClick: zoom moves focus to the svg; pin moves focus to the tooltip's first link. Remember which marker opened the tooltip so that Esc or × returns focus there, falling back to the svg if a re-cluster unmounted it. Draw the focus ring as an extra SVG circle shown on .mk:focus-visible, not only as a CSS outline.
4) Tests. Update render.test.ts:36-41 (the D7 tipTags patch now forces the health tone onto the stage tag), :100-101 (ui.closed vs the prototype golden), :112, :140-165 (override the tag tone from the golden with the stage tone) and :179 ('Clos / en attente' becomes 'Clos / annulé'), each with an L-7 comment. Add a static render assertion for role, tabIndex and aria-label. To test the Enter/Space behaviour, either move the key-to-action rule into a pure function in src/domain/worldmap.ts with a unit test, or add a Playwright check in tests/e2e/06-portfolio.spec.ts. Do not add jsdom or testing-library for this.

Risks: Interpretation: L-7 (a) never says 'register colours' outright; that wording belongs to option (b). Moving the stage tag to the stage colour is inferred from (a) differing from (c), 'health (current)'. Record it as an L-7 deviation in DELIVERY-8 so the owner can confirm. · The grey 'closed' marker also covers the 'other' category: an execution or Garantie project whose healthOf() is null because it has no progress or actuals yet (worldmap.ts:57-60, health.ts:65-68). Under a 'Clos / annulé' legend such a project is mislabelled. The seed has none, but delivery 7 computes health from activities, so a real project can reach execution with a null health. Flag it, or ask the owner whether it needs its own style. · Clusters are keyed by member codes and re-form whenever the zoom level k changes (wheel, view buttons, keyboard zoom). A focused marker <g> can then unmount and drop focus to the body, so focus restoration has to tolerate a missing opener. · CSS outline on SVG <g> elements does not render in every browser (historically not in Safari); a ring drawn as an SVG circle is safer. · Making the markers focusable adds one tab stop per visible cluster (7 at the world view, more when zoomed in). Without arrow-key navigation, a keyboard user tabs through them all before reaching the view buttons. · The golden tip tests compare tag tones with the prototype's tagClass output (render.test.ts:162 takes the EN golden class). Every row's expected tone has to be recomputed from lifecycleTone, so the prototype comparison no longer covers tones; say so in the test comment. · A document-level Esc listener (l.279-286) already exists; a marker-level Esc handler must not double-handle the key or steal focus when the tooltip was opened by mouse hover.

#### R-136 — Portfolio schedule: sliding 24-month window, quarter steps (buildable, M)

Sliding 24-month window with quarter steps for the Portfolio schedule, following owner answer L-8 (a), with "now" = PG_NOW until R-013.
1) Domain, src/domain/pfplan.ts:
- Window start `from` is a month offset since Jan 2026 and a multiple of 3.
- defaultWindowStart(now = PG_NOW) = 3·round((now−12)/3) = −3 on the seed, i.e. Oct 2025 – Sep 2027.
- pgPct, pgSeg and pgRow take `from` (default 0) and clamp to [from, from+24].
- clipText when fc > from+24.
- todayL is null when now is outside the window.
- pgMonths(from) labels MO[((from+i)%12+12)%12] and shows the year on each January and on the first column.
- portfolioScheduleModel(input, { filter, lang, from }) returns months, todayL | null and window { from, label, prevFrom, nextFrom, isDefault }.
- Board states, gates, "Decision pending" and the KPIs stay on PG_NOW.
2) URL, page.tsx: parse ?from=YYYY-MM. It must be a quarter month (Jan/Apr/Jul/Oct) within the chosen bounds, else the default applies. Filter links keep `from`; window links keep `filter`.
3) UI: a Period group in the existing FilterBar of view.tsx, made of server-rendered links: Previous quarter, Today (drops ?from), Next quarter, and the window label. PortfolioGantt draws the today line only when todayL is set, and its doc comments are updated.
4) format.ts fmtD: the year suffix comes from the actual year. Values for 2026 and 2027 are unchanged; this also affects portfolio.ts:239/267.
5) Optional: the export provider adds a "Period" filter line. Its sheets do not change.
6) Remove PG_MONTHS from pipeline.ts only together with src/server/dal/delivery3.test.ts, or keep it.
7) Tests:
- Every golden comparison pins from = 0 (pfplan.test.ts, pfplan.render.test.ts, pfplan.scenarios.test.ts), with a comment citing L-8. The golden generator script does not change.
- New cases: default window, quarter steps, clipping on both sides, today outside the window, URL parsing and bounds, fmtD for 2028, and the navigation links.
- i18n strings go in src/i18n/extra/pfplan.json; "Period" already exists in fr-dict.

Risks: The default view moves away from the prototype (Oct 2025 – Sep 2027 instead of Jan 2026 – Dec 2027). Projects finishing Oct–Dec 2027 become clipped by default, stage-history segments from late 2025 become visible, and the today line moves from 37.38% to about 49.9%. This is what L-8 asks for, but render-test expectations change. · With the default left at from = 0 in pgPct, pgSeg, pgRow and the model, a page that forgets to pass `from` silently shows the old fixed window. With the default set to defaultWindowStart instead, about a dozen golden assertions in three test files fail unless they pin from = 0. · Removing PG_MONTHS from pipeline.ts breaks src/server/dal/delivery3.test.ts, which is not in the triage file list. · monthDate() is wrong for negative month offsets. Any new label or date derived through it for the Oct–Dec 2025 columns would be wrong; use mod arithmetic. · 'Now' stays the frozen PG_NOW, so the window does not slide over real time until R-013 (waiting for S-4) brings the real clock. · The allowed range (Jan 2020 – Oct 2035), the round-to-nearest-quarter centring and the year on the first column are Keystone defaults, not owner data. They should be listed as deviations in the delivery notes. · The FR window label ('oct. 2025 – sept. 2027') must go through t() and the month dictionary. Typed text is not involved. · Reading `from` in the export provider is cosmetic unless a 'Period' filter line is added.

#### R-149 — Notifier les personnes mentionnées par @ dans les commentaires de tâche (buildable, M)

1. Parser in src/domain/mentions.ts, with unit tests. Find '@Full Name' and '@J. Wagner' tokens, longest match first, ignoring case and accents. Match them against a list of persons with their full name, which can be null, and their short name. Return the distinct person ids. The sheet reuses the same function to highlight mentions.

2. After the comment is committed, outside its transaction, without undoing the comment if it fails (like notifyPersons in src/server/dal/client.ts):
   - Resolve the mentions among persons with an active internal account. Exclude users with isExternal or the 'client' role, and the author.
   - Call createNotification with:
     - kind 'mention'
     - title '@you in T-xxxx'
     - sub '“excerpt of at most 120 characters” · author'
     - screen 'task', params { taskId }
     - projectCode = the task's project
     - audience [], toPersonIds
   - Add one audit line ('mentioned …').
   - Add the French rule /^@you in (T-\d+)$/ → '@vous dans $1' to src/i18n/fr-rules.ts, and check that no earlier rule catches the title first.

3. Fix deepLink in src/server/dal/shell.ts so that a 'task' target passes params.taskId to screenHref and does not repeat it in the query string. Add a test that the bell opens the right task.

4. On the task sheet, highlight mentions in posted comments, and add a small suggestion list after '@':
   - It lists internal people with an account, the task's project team first (listProjectTeams / getNamedTeam).
   - Keys: ↑↓, Enter, Esc.
   - It inserts '@Full Name'.
   - The page passes the list to the sheet.
   - The design system has no combobox, so this is new UI built from Popover.

5. Strings in a new i18n extra file registered in src/i18n/extra/index.ts.

6. Tests:
   - DAL: the person mentioned gets exactly one unread mention linking to /my/tasks/T-xxxx; the author, a client and an unknown name get nothing.
   - The sheet still renders, and so do its suggestions.

E-mail stays with R-036.

Risks: Wrong task opened: the deep link would point at T-2314 (src/server/dal/shell.ts:357-367 with src/config/screens.ts:152) unless deepLink is fixed. The scope does not list this file. · Creating the notification inside addTaskComment's transaction could block on PGlite, or duplicate the notification when R-010 retries the transaction. It must run after the commit, and a failure must not undo the comment. · The suggestion list after '@' is not in the prototype; only the placeholder and notification n6 are. It is the part closest to the 'no new tools' rule. It is defensible as the way to use the validated mentions, but keep it minimal or confirm it with the owner. The design system has no combobox, so it is built from scratch, with keyboard and accessibility work, and the effort is more than M. · Open rights question in docs/delivery-7/notes-rights.md §8: should the task sheet be restricted? Internal roles read any task today. If the sheet is restricted later, people mentioned from outside the project would get the excerpt in their bell but could not open the task. Mentionable people should be filtered by the same rule. · The excerpt is copied into notifications.body. Later edits or deletions of the comment do not update it, and fieldsText splits it on ' · ', so a segment that looks like a number or date (e.g. '12 Oct') can still be translated. · fr-rules.ts is labelled 'Generated from the prototype' and has not changed since delivery 2. A rule added there needs a comment, and rule order matters because the first matching rule wins. · Persons may have no full name (persons.fullName is nullable), and short names like 'J. Wagner' must still match. Two people with the same name, and names that are prefixes of others, need the longest-match rule and tests. · In entra mode only people who have already signed in have an account, so others cannot be mentioned. The mention stays plain text and nobody is told, which is the agreed default.

#### R-015 — Notifier les événements clés aux personnes concernées (X-6a) (partly buildable, L)

Bell notifications in Keystone only (X-6a), through one recipients helper built on createNotification with toPersonIds. The helper maps persons to their active user accounts, never notifies the actor, and leaves client users to the existing read-time filter (dal/shell.ts:389-405). Every notice is sent after the writer's transaction commits, from the Server Function or job. A failure is logged with logError and never undoes the write.

Live now:
(1) CR submitted (features/changes/actions.ts after submitChangeRequest). Either build R-069 (sequential chain P-6b) first and notify the approver of the first pending step, then the next step's approver after each decision, or, without R-069, notify the approver of every pending step at submit.
- PM step: the PM of record (default from notes-rights §6 Q4).
- Director step: users with the director role.
- Client step: client users of the project's client organisation (none at launch).
- CCB step: the director (P-6a), not a Settings value.
- On final approval or rejection: the requester (requestedByPersonId).
(2) Delivery refused: after recordReceiptQc or rejectReceipt with rejected units > 0, notify the project leads and the PO's creator or submitter, else the procurement users.
(3) Invoice blocked: invoices of the receipt or its PO whose matchedInvoiceStatus turns from 'awaiting_receipt' to 'blocked' after QC (compare before and after). Notify the PO creator or submitter, else the procurement users, plus the director (prototype n8).
(4) @mention (also closes R-149): parse '@First Last' or '@F. Last' in addTaskComment against persons with an active account and notify the person mentioned. Unknown names are ignored.
(5) Task assigned: tasks created from meeting actions (dal/meetings.ts) and activity to-dos (dal/activity-todos.ts) notify the assignee, unless the assignee is the actor. Tasks born from requests already do this.
(6) KPI alert: new job 'kpi-alerts' (hourly) in src/domain/jobs.ts and src/server/jobs/definitions.ts. It runs getPortfolioAlerts('en') with ALERT_RULE_DEFAULTS and the health thresholds in Settings, at the current data date (Friday freeze waits for S-4). A new table, added with a generated migration, records the notified alert keys. A new key notifies once; it can notify again only after it clears and reappears.
- Project alerts go to the project leads and the director.
- Portfolio-wide alerts (over-allocation, approvals waiting) go to the director.
- The first run needs a baseline so it does not flood the bell with every alert already active.

Dormant until Vigie publishes its API (V-3), built as an isolated hook in refreshFromVigie of the HTTP connectors only (diff before and after the upsert, never inside upsertNcrMirror or upsertMirror, which the seed also calls), and covered by tests with injected records:
(7) NCR raised: notify the project leads, plus the quality referent and engineering lead when they are on the project team.
(8) ECO entering in_approval: notify each pending key holder. The pm key goes to the leads of the affected projects (leadProjectCodes); the quality and englead keys go to users holding those roles.

French texts go in src/i18n/extra/d8-notifications.json. Titles are fixed templates; typed text stays in the sub-line.

Tests per event: recipient set, actor excluded, client exclusion, dedup.

Do not mark R-015 'fait' for events (7) and (8) until the Vigie API exists. Update R-149 and R-121 in reste-a-developper.json.

Risks: createNotification opens its own db.transaction (dal/notifications.ts:103-118). Called from inside a writer's transaction (recordReceiptQc, the Vigie refresh transaction) it can block on PGlite's single connection. With R-010 transaction retries it can also send the same notice twice. Emit notifications only after commit. · The bell passes titles through t() (components/app/shell/page-header.tsx:77). A title holding typed text (CR title, project name in a KPI alert, task title) would be segment-translated, against R-148. Keep titles as fixed English templates and put typed text in the sub-line (fieldsText). · The KPI alert job: getPortfolioAlerts works at DEFAULT_DATA_DATE = STATUS_ISO (S-4 open) with code-default thresholds ('Alert thresholds in Settings' is open in DELIVERY-7 §8). Alerts change only when the data changes. The first run would notify every alert already active (Harbor Line etc.) unless a baseline is stored first. · In getPortfolioAlerts, directorQueue takes the identity from PERSONA_BY_ROLE.director. Run from the worker in Entra mode, it may not match the real director's approval queue. · CR routing depends on R-069 (sequential chain P-6b), which is not built. Without it, either notify all pending approvers at once (as the prototype n1 does: director and client) or build R-069 first. Routing the PM step to every PM or deputy is an open question (notes-rights §6 Q4). · NCR and ECO notifications are dead code in mock mode, the only mode that works today. Marking R-015 'fait' would overstate what works; give them a progress note that they wait on Vigie (V-3). · NCR recipients beyond the leads depend on project team roles. The demo referents exist only on Harbor Line, and real teams wait for the owner's RACI matrix. Fall back to the project leads. · No buyer field on purchase_orders: createdBy or submittedBy may be null on seeded POs, so the fallback to all procurement users will be common. · The bell already holds the seeded prototype notifications (n1-n10), so live and demo notices will be mixed in demo databases. · The notification panel footer still advertises an e-mail digest and Teams (R-036, X-6b). It stays wrong until the mail relay exists; this item is out of scope for that.

#### R-150 — Changer le statut d'une tâche par glisser-déposer dans le Tableau (buildable, S)

The proposed scope is buildable as it stands, with three adjustments:
1. Change the card markup. The 'Move to…' Popover trigger is a <button>, and a <button> cannot sit inside the current <Link> card. When canEdit is true, render the card as a div with a click-to-open handler and an inner title Link, the same pattern as TaskRowItem with the INTERACTIVE guard. When canEdit is false, keep the plain <Link> card.
2. Change the reducer signature. useOptimistic becomes a small action union ({kind:'toggle',id} | {kind:'status',id,status}) that applies toggleDone or applyStatus. Update the List's flip(id) call to match.
3. Put the new strings in a JSON file under src/i18n/extra/: mywork.json, or a new d8-*.json registered in index.ts. index.ts on its own is not where entries go.
Unchanged from the triage:
- HTML5 drag with a custom MIME type for the task id, effectAllowed 'move', and a column highlight on dragover/dragleave.
- A drop on the same column, or with an unchanged status, does nothing.
- On error, the optimistic state rolls back through revalidation and toast.showEn shows the error.
- The keyboard/touch 'Move to…' menu lists the other three statuses. Focus returns to the card and a toast confirms ('Moved to {status}').
- Read-only board when canEdit is false.
- Order inside a column stays by due date.
- Render tests: draggable attributes and drop handler, the menu, the read-only case. Update the existing 'board and calendar views' assertions in mywork-render.test.ts if the text changes.
- Then follow the standing rule: update docs/reste-a-developper.json, run npm run remaining:page and republish the page.

Risks: Invalid HTML and hydration risk if the Popover trigger button is put inside the existing <Link> card. The card needs restructuring (div + inner Link + row-click guard), and that must not break the read-only 'click opens the task' behaviour or the existing render-test text assertions. · HTML5 drag-and-drop gotchas. dragover needs preventDefault or drop never fires. dragleave flickers when the pointer passes over child elements. A dragged <a> also carries its URL by default, which can trigger browser navigation when dropped elsewhere. Use a custom dataTransfer type and ignore drops that lack it. · Touch support is uneven: iPadOS Safari supports draggable, Android Chrome is limited. The 'Move to…' menu is the real path on tablets (R-146 layouts), so it must be reachable and large enough to tap at tablet width. · The useOptimistic reducer goes from a bare id to an action union. The List done toggle (flip(id)) must be migrated in the same change or it stops being optimistic. · revalidatePath('/', 'layout') refreshes the whole layout after each drop. Several quick drops can briefly flash the old state between optimistic and server state. Acceptable, but test it with a slow action. · This deliberately departs from the prototype (spec 10 §6.4 'No drag-and-drop; no toggle'). Note it in the delivery notes with decision id R-8c so prototype-parity reviewers do not flag it. · A director viewing someone else's board (?person=) can move their cards. This matches canEditTask and R-9 = b, but should be stated in the delivery notes. A PM viewing a team member's board stays read-only. · Accessibility: menu items need clear names (e.g. 'Move to In review'), the confirmation must be announced (toast live region), and focus must go back to the moved card even after it re-renders in a different column. Key the focus by task id, not by a DOM node.

#### R-067 — Créer les tâches de suivi des actions de compte rendu avec l'échéance saisie (buildable, M)

Same as the triage scope, with these corrections:
(1) Migration generated after 0015: meeting_lines.activity_id uuid null → activities.id, on delete set null. collaboration.ts imports activities from ./planning.
(2) Composer, action type only: an optional Activity select, loaded only when canWrite (never for a client viewer). Options in plan order, labels shown with dataText (R-148). parseMinutesLine accepts `activity` (a code). appendLines and addMeetingLine resolve the code inside m.projectId and reject an activity of another project. Transcript-applied lines get no activity.
(3) Pure target rule in src/domain/meetings.ts:
- An internal person (persons.client_id null) gets the task.
- Any other owner is external. Find the project's stakeholder by person_id, else by exact full name or short form, after stripping a trailing '(Org)'. Assign to that stakeholder's owner_person_id.
- Fall back to projects.pm_person_id also when the matched stakeholder has no owner_person_id. With no PM, create no task.
- If several stakeholders match, take the first by position.
- Title: action text + ' — ' + the owner as typed. Labels: Meeting, MTG-xxx, Follow-up.
(4) Dates:
- due = meeting_lines.due_date, else TODAY+3 working days;
- start = TODAY, or the due date when the typed due is earlier;
- activityId from the line; origin 'meeting'; meeting_lines.task_id set; audit and toast count include follow-ups.
(5) Test updates:
- delivery3-writes.test.ts:197-210: tasks become [T-2341 L. Fabre, T-2342 J. Wagner, T-2343 H. Sauvage]. agenda[1] task numbers become [T-2342, T-2343]. J. Wagner's task is T-2342 with due 2026-10-01. tasksBefore + 3.
- meetings.golden.test.ts:250 toHaveLength(3); :256 expects r.tasks[1], not r.tasks[0]. Comment cites S-12.
- actions.test.ts:123: the toast becomes '3 actions added to tasks', and the test title no longer says 'pool owners'.
- shell.test.ts:167 and meetings-transcripts.test.ts:189 are not affected.
- New tests: N. Bensaïd → R. Aubert, on a new meeting because MTG-117 is already sent; 'K. Lindqvist (Nordvolt)' → F. Bertrand; unknown external owner → PM; matched stakeholder without an owner → PM; activity carried to the task; '—' due → +3 working days; activity of another project refused.
- i18n: 'Activity', 'Follow-up'.
No backfill for minutes already sent (MTG-116/117).

Risks: Giving L. Fabre (internal, outside the resource pool) a task is a reading of S-12 ('Keystone crée une tâche pour chaque responsable interne'), not a literal ask. It changes the toast count shown for MTG-118 from 2 to 3. Record it as a default in the delivery notes. · The proposed test plan misses three changes. actions.test.ts:123 hard-codes '2 actions added to tasks'. meetings.golden.test.ts:256 checks agenda[1].lines[1] against r.tasks[0], which becomes L. Fabre's task. delivery3-writes.test.ts:205-206 assume T-2341 is J. Wagner's; it becomes L. Fabre's. · Year-less typed dues are parsed with defaultYear 2026 (parseMeetingDate / isoOfDisplayDate). Now that the typed due drives the task, a '5 Jan' typed in late 2026, or anything typed in 2027, gives a past due and a past start. Consider rolling the year forward from the meeting date. · Keeping a past typed due differs from the activity to-do precedent, which clamps due to STATUS_ISO (activity-todos.ts:76). This is a deliberate choice under S-12 ('reprend l'échéance saisie'); document it. · The internal/external test relies only on persons.client_id. A client user whose person row has no client_id (users.client_id set at sign-in or by an admin) would be treated as internal and receive the task. Also check users.is_external. · The client sees external meetings with their lines' taskNumber and taskHref (model.ts:271). The client's own action would now show the number of an internal follow-up task assigned to the PM. Consider hiding task links for client viewers. · Meeting tasks linked to an activity will appear in the Plan drawer's to-do list (listActivityTodos filters only on activityId). They are not removable there because their origin is 'meeting'. This is probably desirable, but it is new visible behaviour. · A task is still created for an action already ticked done before sending (prototype behaviour), so the assignee gets an open 'todo' for finished work. · Activity labels and follow-up titles are typed text and must be shown with dataText, never t(), per R-148. Follow-up titles may also exceed the 200 characters that the activity to-do composer allows.

#### R-037 — Synchroniser les réunions Outlook / Teams vers Keystone (S-11) (partly buildable, S)

Labels that tell the truth, plus read receipts recorded inside Keystone. The sync from Outlook stays out until IT registers Keystone in Microsoft 365 with Graph access (I-3c).

1) Meeting header (src/features/meetings/model.ts:313, meetings-view.tsx:195): show the series alone. Show 'Synced from Outlook · {series}' only when meetings.externalCalendarId is set; no meeting has it today. Update meetings.golden.test.ts:57 with a comment citing S-11 and A-15.

Never edit an existing migration.
- 'Microsoft Teams': requalify it (scope text: Teams meetings through the Microsoft 365 app with Outlook, no channel posts per X-6b; stays 'Not connected') rather than adding it to RETIRED_CONNECTORS. Retire it only after the owner confirms, because I-3c=a keeps Teams.
- Either way, notificationChannels() (src/domain/admin.ts:269-284) stops counting Teams as a channel (X-6b). The sub-line becomes 'In-app · e-mail not connected'. Update admin.test.ts and the admin and delivery5 goldens citing X-6b and S-11.

- It calls recordMinutesRead, changed to match meeting_participants.personId (falling back on the name), only when the participant is a minutes recipient and minutesReadOn is still null. Audit, then revalidatePath.
