List the Keystone-only receipts and the Teams row choice as deviations in the delivery notes, with an open question to the owner.

Risks: I-3c=a explicitly keeps Teams ('Outlook, Teams et e-mails' in one Microsoft app). Retiring the 'Microsoft Teams' row the way Lucca was retired is not backed by an answer; only Teams as a notification channel is dropped (X-6b). Requalify the row or ask the owner first. · viewer.personId is null for all demo personas (src/config/personas.ts:24-25). Keying receipts literally on viewer.personId would record nothing in demo mode, development or tests. Resolve the person by short name the way the connectors do. · If the Server Function for receipts reuses the meetings write guard (editable()), it will refuse read-only viewers and closed projects. Reading the minutes must stay allowed. · Recording a read when someone simply views the meeting detail overstates reading, and a mutation during a Server Component render is not allowed. Trigger it only from the 'Open minutes' action, or from opening the MIN record. · Minutes recipients get no notification or e-mail when the minutes are sent (no notify in sendMinutes; e-mail is R-036). Keystone-only receipts will mostly stay 'Not read yet' until R-036 or X-6a notifications exist. Opening the document in Codex itself is never seen. · The connector scope texts are stored rows seeded from PROTO.CONNECTORS. Changing only the seed leaves databases already seeded unchanged, so it needs a backfill or an override at read time. The goldens (admin.golden.json, delivery5.golden.json) and fr-dict.json:2558-2559 must be updated with comments citing the decisions. · Other false labels stay unless they are added to the scope: 'Create & invite', the 'minutes sent to {n} invitees' toast, the composer footer 'read receipts are tracked', and the bell footer that names the e-mail digest and the Teams channel (R-036). · Keystone-only read receipts are a product choice that no answer explicitly asks for: the S-11 question tied receipts to the Microsoft 365 connector. Record it as a deviation and an open question in the delivery 8 notes.

#### R-089 — Envoyer aux achats une tâche pour trouver l’intervenant externe (partly buildable, M)

Buildable now:

1. Routing to procurement. On approve (approveResourceRequest, reached from both the Approvals queue and My approvals) and on accept-reschedule of a request with no named person (person_id null, contract_role set), the task is created and assigned to procurement:
   - Assignee: the first active user holding the procurement role (user sort order); the director if there is none.
   - Title: 'Find and name: <contract role> — <request title>', with the same dates, hours and source request.
   - Notified: every active procurement user and the requesting PM.
   - The audit and activity text read "task created for Procurement".
   The invalid notice to r.person (null) is removed.

2. Procurement's access. Add 'mywork' to procurement's ROLE_SCREENS (canSee maps 'task' to 'mywork'). This is a P-8b consequence default, like R-061 for Plan, and is listed as an owner question. Update the route-matrix and role tests.

3. "Name the person" on the task sheet, for procurement and the director, only on a task created from a request that has a contract role and no person yet:
   - Choose an EXISTING person only: an internal pool person or an existing external contact.
   - The same task is reassigned: assignee_person_id is set and contract_role cleared.
   - resource_requests.person_id is set; the request keeps contract_role as history.
   - Records the audit row '<T-number> · named: <person>', the activity, and an event on the request.
   - Notifies the PM, and the person when they have a user account.
   - Revalidates the app layout.
   If the person is not yet in Keystone, the task stays with procurement, with a comment.

4. DAL writer. A task reassignment writer in src/server/dal/tasks.ts (or requests.ts) that re-checks the actor's role, the task's origin and the not-yet-named state, inside one transaction.

5. i18n (fr-dict) and tests: requests actions, the tasks DAL, task sheet rendering, procurement routing on both approve paths and accept-reschedule, and updated delivery2, approvals and myapprovals expectations for RQ-043.

Not buildable now: creating a new external person (name, company, e-mail) from Keystone. It waits on the Cursus staff directory (NEW-CURSUS-2 (a), R-027) and on the owner's answer on how people enter Keystone (DELIVERY-7 §8 auth). No new persons column or migration.

Risks: Procurement's workspace has no My work/Task today (src/config/role-screens.ts). Without adding 'mywork' or hosting the action elsewhere, procurement gets OUTSIDE refusals from src/features/mywork/actions.ts gate(). Adding it changes the prototype's role gating and must be flagged as an owner question. · Once a task is named to an external person without a user account, only the director can update it (canEditTask = director or assignee). The PM and procurement lose edit rights on its status, % and time unless the rule is widened. This needs a stated default. · While the task sits with procurement it carries the request's estimate hours (e.g. 40 h). This inflates procurement's My work load and any future Cursus task read (BACKLOG §1 bis). Consider keeping the request hours but flagging the task as sourcing. · Hand-created persons would bypass the Cursus directory chosen in NEW-CURSUS-2 (a). Their e-mail is the Entra sign-in key (users.ts:124-129), and matching by e-mail is fragile for temporary workers who have no professional address. This is why that part is excluded. · The first-procurement-user pick is arbitrary when several users hold the role, and the director fallback must be tested in Entra mode, where users come from real sign-ins. · Existing tests assert the contract-role assignee text and toasts (approvals.test.ts, delivery2*.test.ts, ch4-decisions.test.ts, plan drawer-model.test.ts, home.test.ts). They need deliberate updates citing P-8b. · The naming action is new UI absent from the prototype. It is justified only by the P-8b answer, so it should stay minimal: a person picker, no supplier or RFQ workflow (option b was not chosen).

#### R-090 — Allow re-requesting a withdrawn resource request (P-8c) (buildable, S)

The proposed scope is buildable as written.

(1) src/features/requests/actions.ts reRequestAction: accept 'rejected' or 'withdrawn'. Every other status, including draft, approved and reschedule, still returns STALE. Keep ownRequest (canManageOwnRequests plus isRequester) and the existing "New resource request" notification and "Re-sent resource request" activity. Change the doc comment to "(rejected or withdrawn)". The DAL needs no change.

(2) src/features/requests/model.ts: add a canReRequest flag (status rejected or withdrawn) to RequestRowView and keep isRejected.

(3) Requests screen:
- requests-screen.tsx:100-101: show RequestRowActions when the row is mine and pending, reschedule or canReRequest, and pass the new prop.
- request-row-actions.tsx: show "Re-request" on that flag and update the header comment, citing P-8c.

(4) Tests:
- actions.test.ts:92: expect { ok: true }, then status pending, response '', sentOn today, with a comment citing P-8c (R-090).
- model.test.ts: a withdrawn request of mine offers Re-request; one sent by someone else does not.
- Optionally, delivery2-writes.test.ts: withdraw → re-request ending in events [..., 'withdrawn', 'resent'].

(5) Docs: update the progress in reste-a-developper.json, run npm run remaining:page and republish the remaining-work page, and add a line in the delivery notes.

Optional, for the owner to confirm before it is added: a getProjectScope().readOnly guard on reRequestAction. It would apply to both rejected and withdrawn requests and would change today's behaviour for rejected ones.

Risks: The re-request keeps the dates the request was sent with. A request withdrawn weeks ago goes back to the department head with past start and due dates (nrDates are only computed when the request is first sent). The head has to propose a reschedule, as for a rejected request today. The owner's answer does not cover editing a request before re-sending it. · reRequestAction (like withdraw and accept-reschedule) does not check getProjectScope().readOnly. CLAUDE.md asks every Server Function to apply the read-only rules, so with this change a PM taken off the project team (R-041), or a PM on a read-only project, can revive a withdrawn request. Adding the guard is consistent with the convention, but it also changes the existing rejected path, so it should be called out in the notes or confirmed with the owner. · The request keeps its stored department and departmentHead. If the person moved to another department since, the re-request goes to the old department head, who decides it under P-8a (R-048). The same is already true for rejected re-requests. · After a re-request from the 'Rejected / withdrawn' chip (?status=rejected), the row leaves the filtered list once the page is revalidated. That is expected but could surprise users; no confirmation toast is planned, matching the prototype. · The test assertion at actions.test.ts:92 (comment '08 OQ21: only rejected') must be flipped, not deleted. The seeded counts (chip 'Rejected / withdrawn 1') do not change because the seed has no withdrawn request, so model, drafts and render tests should stay green. · The evidence line numbers in reste-a-developper.json (actions.ts:146-150) are out of date; the current lines are actions.ts:150-166. Update them when writing progress.note.

Keep the existing 'Request sent · RQ-045' (FR 'Demande envoyée · RQ-045'), or add a requests i18n helper for 'Request RQ-045 sent' / 'Demande RQ-045 envoyée' (the owner's example) in src/features/requests/i18n.ts.

Risks: Static render tests (renderToStaticMarkup, node environment) cannot exercise a toast that appears after an awaited Server Function; coverage has to come from pure helpers plus Playwright e2e. · When the portfolio director forces a move, the toast tells them validation was requested from the portfolio director, i.e. from themselves. L-1 still creates a pending validation for them in My approvals, so it is accurate but may read oddly. A role-aware wording such as 'Forced stage change recorded · awaiting validation in My approvals' could be considered, but keep the owner's example by default. · The forced move is applied optimistically (useOptimistic). The toast must be shown only after r.ok, never before the server result, or a stale or refused move would be confirmed. · Using showEn for the new strings would push codes and typed names through t() again (the R-148 regression). Build the strings with toast.show plus t() on Keystone's own vocabulary only, and dataText for names. · The dictionaries test enforces one French translation per delivery-7 key across every dictionary. A new key must not duplicate a different FR rendering elsewhere, and a new JSON file must be registered in src/i18n/extra/index.ts. · Changing the request toast wording from 'Request sent · RQ-xxx' to 'Request RQ-xxx sent' touches an existing string. Any existing e2e or unit assertion on the old text must be updated.

#### R-064 — Corbeille du Plan : confirmer rétention et droits, purge planifiée (partly buildable, S)

Build now (technical, no owner input):

(1) Correct place after a restore, with no duplicate positions. Use the "ghost slot" rule: trashed wbs_nodes and activities rows keep their place in the project's shared position sequence.
- insertActivity shifts the positions stored in plan_trash payloads of the same project (wbs_nodes and activities rows) at or above the new position, in the same transaction as the live rows.
- insertWorkPackage computes its position as max(live, stored in trash) + 1.
- restorePlanTrash then re-inserts at the stored positions, which stay free and in order.
- This keeps a restore byte-identical when nothing changed. It also keeps the chained test at plan-trash.test.ts:138-146 green, which the scope's anchor-only rule breaks.

Alternative: an anchor rule that stores both neighbours (previous and next row) and prefers the stored slot when it is free and lies between them. Defensive fallback in either design: if a stored slot is taken anyway, shift the rows at or above it by the block size before inserting.

(2) Restored activity_links whose (successor, position) is taken (successor's predecessors rewritten by updateActivity in the meantime) go to max + 1 for that successor.

(3) Move PLAN_TRASH_RETENTION_DAYS and PLAN_TRASH_MAX_ENTRIES physically into src/domain/ch4-defaults.ts, or into the pure src/domain/jobs.ts and re-export them from ch4-defaults, under an S-5 heading. plan-trash.ts then imports them (and may re-export them for its tests). The rights rule stays where it is, with a comment pointing to canEditPlanStructure / canSchedulePlan. Never import plan-trash.ts or actions.ts from ch4-defaults.

(4) Tests:
- Remove A1250, insert an activity in an earlier package and one in package 1.4, restore. Positions must be unique and A1250 back between A1240 and A1260; moveActivity must swap it.
- Same for a work package removed while it is the last one, then a new package added.
- Link position after the successor's predecessors were edited.
- Keep the existing 15-table comparisons green.

Files: src/server/dal/plan-trash.ts, src/server/dal/plan.ts, src/domain/ch4-defaults.ts (or src/domain/jobs.ts), src/server/dal/plan-trash.test.ts, src/features/plan/actions.test.ts.

Not buildable (owner): confirming 30 days, 20 entries and PM + director only (DELIVERY-6 §8 S-5). Keep these defaults until then.

Risks: The proposed anchor rule (place the block right after the row that preceded it at removal) gives the wrong order when neighbouring rows are removed one after the other and restored in removal order. It fails the existing test plan-trash.test.ts:138-146 (A1250 then A1260, dump() must equal before). Use ghost slots, or anchors on both sides with the stored slot preferred. · Re-exporting the constants from server-only plan-trash.ts into ch4-defaults.ts would pull 'server-only' into the 'use client' islands that import ch4-defaults (plumbing-islands.tsx, transcript-client.tsx, photo-gallery.tsx) and break next build. The constants must move into a pure module. · With ghost slots, insertActivity and insertWorkPackage have to read and rewrite the project's plan_trash payloads: at most 20 small JSON rows, but it couples plan.ts to the trash format. Every future writer of positions must do the same. The schedule importer (src/server/interchange/schedule-import.ts) is off today (D-7b IMPORTS_ENABLED=false) and would need it if imports into an existing project are turned on. · No lock serialises position writers on PostgreSQL. Two concurrent inserts or restores on one project can still collide. Locking the projects row with .for('update') at the start of insertActivity, insertWorkPackage, trashPlanRow and restorePlanTrash, as other DAL writers do, would serialise them. · handOver moves rows between trash entries. Positions travel with the rows, so ghost shifting must cover every entry of the project, not only the one being restored. · The limits (30 days, 20 entries) and the PM + director rule are still unconfirmed (DELIVERY-6 §8 S-5). Mark R-064 'fait' only for the technical part. The item stays 'en_cours' until the owner confirms.

#### R-172 — Solder les TODO(db) restants (import P&ID, lien CR ↔ activité, nom du responsable) (partly buildable, S)

1) DAL, src/server/dal/plan.ts:105,150. Select owner.shortName as well, and set each activity's ownerName = fullName ?? shortName. Without this the wizard's default PM S. Perrin, whose full_name is NULL, still shows 'SP'.
2) Show that name wherever the plan shows an owner. Rule: `a.ownerName ?? owners.get(a.owner) ?? a.owner`, in:
   - src/features/plan/model.ts:111;

3) OwnerSelect (src/features/plan/plan-table.tsx:347).

4) Replace the stale TODO(db) at src/server/dal/pid-import.ts:9-10 with a note: the staging is re-planned from the submitted file inside the apply transaction by design, and an ImportJob (kind 'pid') waits for D-7b. Change no import behaviour.
5) Tests.

   - On a wizard project whose PM has a full name but no owner_code, they show that full name.

6) docs/reste-a-developper.json.

   - The P&ID ImportJob part stays deferred to D-7b.

Flag these gaps but do not build them:
- Owner candidates are limited to persons with an owner_code (plan.ts:120-122), which gives an empty list under KEYSTONE_SEED=production.
- 'Add activity' falls back to owner 'RA' (actions.ts:258).
- A team member who has no owner_code cannot enter progress on their own activity (actions.ts:67). The follow-up is owners from the project team (R-041), keyed by person.

Risks: As scoped, the rule only reads persons.full_name. The canonical 'SP' case (S. Perrin, the wizard's default PM) has full_name NULL, so without the DAL short-name fallback the fix silently does nothing and test (4) fails or gets written to the wrong expectation. · The owner key is `ownerCode ?? initials` (plan.ts:150) and initials are not unique (schema/organisation.ts:39). If a PM without an owner_code has initials equal to an OWN code (e.g. 'RA'), a key-only 'is it in the list' check shows and saves the wrong person, and mayEnterProgress (actions.ts:67) can grant progress entry to the OWN holder. The demo seed has no such collision today. · Activities with no owner (MS Project / P6 XER imports, production-seed Add activity) also make OwnerSelect show the first owner. Leave them out and the editable-plan bug stays half-fixed. · Under KEYSTONE_SEED=production the owner list is empty. The injected option makes the select show the right owner, but no reassignment is possible until owners come from the project team (R-041 follow-up). · Creating a wizard project inside providers.test.ts or the plan tests can change the counts or order other tests in the same file rely on. Prefer a separate in-memory database or the ch4-settings.test.ts setup. · Widening PlanRow.act / DrawerInput.act touches domain types shared by the schedule and export code. A per-id lookup in data.activities avoids changing the domain types. · The P&ID comment change must stay a comment. Any move to import_jobs (ImportKind 'pid', a migration) would go against D-7b's freeze on imports.

#### R-116 — Dater la réception d’une interface (buildable, S)

Same scope as the triage, with a few files added and some precisions.
- 'Mark received' opens a one-field dialog, 'Received on', using FormDialog with a text date field like 'Needed by'.
- The field's initial value is NOW_ISO (30 Sep 2026), shown in the viewer's language, and the user can edit it.
- The Server Function parses the date with parseDateInput (src/domain/contract.ts:380). It refuses an empty or invalid date with DATE_ERROR, and a date after NOW_ISO with a new FR string. It keeps the current checks: editable('interfaces'), row state canReceive, unknown interface.
- In the DAL, markInterfaceReceived takes the date. transition() (or a receive-specific variant) sets status 'received' and provided_on in the same update.
- The audit line becomes 'status: Late → Received · received 28 Sep'.
- Freeze leaves provided_on untouched.
- Rows received or frozen before this change keep '—'. There is no backfill.
- Remove InterfaceSummary.dueWithin30 and its computation from src/domain/team.ts. In src/domain/team.test.ts, update the golden KPI assertion (:88-91) with a comment citing A-12/Q-4, and the assertions at :98, :115 and :120.
- Fix the stale comments in src/server/dal/interfaces.ts:2-5 and src/server/db/schema/operations.ts:32.
- Keep focus management: after the dialog closes, focus goes to the new 'Freeze' button.

Files to add to the triage's list:
- src/server/dal/shell.test.ts:171 and src/server/dal/delivery3-writes.test.ts:226. Both call markInterfaceReceived(projectCode, number, actor) with three arguments. Either update them, or put the date last as an optional parameter defaulting to NOW_ISO, as addStakeholder already does.
- src/features/team/actions.test.ts:126-133. These tests call the action without a date and expect the old audit text.
- src/features/team/interfaces-view.tsx, which passes the default date and context to InterfaceActions.
- If a new src/i18n/extra/d8-*.json is created, register it in src/i18n/extra/index.ts. The other option is to add the strings to the existing team.json.

No migration.

Risks: Callers missing from the file list: src/server/dal/shell.test.ts:171 and src/server/dal/delivery3-writes.test.ts:226 call markInterfaceReceived with (code, number, actor). If the date becomes the third parameter, the actor string is passed as the date, so typecheck/tests break or the date is wrong. Put the date last (optional, default NOW_ISO) or update both tests. · The 'today' used by writers differs across the app. Interfaces/ITP use NOW_ISO (30 Sep), deliveries and daily reports use STATUS_ISO (25 Sep), and the R-055 stage change uses the project data date. The interfaces KPI 'due within 30 days' counts from the data date (25 Sep), while lateness uses PG_NOW (30 Sep). NOW_ISO fits interface lateness best, but S-4 (open) will have to unify this later. · Changing the audit text ('· received 28 Sep') breaks src/features/team/actions.test.ts:128 and any audit/activity assertions that expect 'status: Late → Received'. The received date must be formatted per language through data-text rules, not through t(). · Removing dueWithin30 removes golden parity with the prototype's 'n due within 30 days' sub-line (G.interfaces.kpis[1].sub). CLAUDE.md requires a comment in the test citing the decision (A-12 / Q-4). · UX regression risk: today 'Mark received' is one click and focus moves to 'Freeze'. With a dialog, focus returns to a trigger that disappears once the row refreshes, and the render test (src/features/team/render.test.ts:195-203) checks the buttons' aria-labels and count. · Whether the date can be edited after receipt is not decided. Q-4 only says the date proposed at receipt can be corrected. The triage's 'no later edit' default should be stated as a default to confirm, not built as a feature. · Received/frozen rows from before this change and from the seed (IF-01 12 Sep, IF-04 29 Sep, IF-03, IF-06 already have provided dates) are unaffected. Interfaces received through the app before this change stay '—', which is accepted (no backfill).

### Item scopes (triage, after the adversarial check)

#### R-117 — Fusionner les réserves ITP et chantier en une liste unique par projet (buildable, M)

One punch list per project (A-6 'a').

**Migration**
- Write the migration as two steps.
- First, a custom migration (`drizzle-kit generate --custom`, as for 0012):
  - For every project that has both registers, renumber the 'site' items after the project's highest 'itp' number. On HLT-026, P-01..P-03 become P-06..P-08.
  - Backfill category B on site items and owner party 'keystone' on ITP items.
- Then the generated DDL: drop the unique key (project_id, register, number) and add the unique key (project_id, number).
- A generated file on its own fails on seeded databases, because both registers have a P-01.
- Photos stay attached through stored_files.owner_id, which is the item's UUID, so renumbering does not move them. Document links ("P-01" free text) keep pointing at the ITP P-01, the NCR-029 item.

**Columns and codes**
- Keep the `register` column and use it as the item's origin: 'itp' = Inspection, 'site' = Site. Label it in codes.ts and rewrite the comments in codes.ts:911-916 and operations.ts.
- Every item has a category, an owner (a person or a party), an optional NCR number (Vigie number only) and an optional source inspection.

**DAL**
- One loadPunch per project, with next = Start or Close for every open item.
- addPunchItem takes the origin and numbers over the whole project. In demo mode the floor of 9 stays, so the next item is P-10, not P-09.
- advancePunchItem works on (project, number) without a register filter.
- openPunchAByProject and Commissioning "Blocking A" count category-A items of every origin.

**Matching items to a system**
- Recommended: keep the prototype's "System / location" free text and its first-word prefix rule (spec 07 §2.5, line 499). Site-origin items get a location from the form.
- A new `system` column chosen from a list is not asked by A-6. Build it only if it is flagged as a deviation.

**Screens**
- Inspections & punch list:
  - Add an Origin column and an Origin filter (All / Inspection / Site).
  - The "Add punch item" form gains the origin, the owner party and an optional NCR.
  - Start / Close on every item.
- Site › Daily execution shows the same list, filtered to origin Site by default with "All" available. Start / Close appears there only if the cross-screen editor default is accepted; otherwise the card stays read-only, as in the prototype.
- KPIs:
  - Site "Punch list open" and "on the client" count the single list.
  - The ITP nav badge and Commissioning count A items of every origin.

**Photos and the client**
- Look the item up by (project, number).
- photoGate and clientSeesPhotos must take the origin from the database row, never from the request's register parameter.
- The client sees the unified list on Inspections & punch list:
  - without NCR numbers, also in the ITP export (control.ts itpWorkbook writes `ncrNumber` today and the client can export the itp screen);
  - without the photos of site-origin items (X-15 still open).

**Exports**
- control.ts:192 and site.ts:74 list the single list; site.ts:55 too.

**Files to add to the triage list**
- src/features/photos/access.ts
- src/app/api/photos/route.ts and [id]/route.ts
- src/features/itpcom/punch-step.tsx, add-buttons.tsx, commissioning-view.tsx
- src/server/dal/shell.ts (comment)
- src/i18n/extra/index.ts, to register d8-quality-site.json

**Tests to update, citing A-6**
- src/server/dal/delivery5-writes.test.ts: it asserts the site register is read-only and that a team member is refused.
- src/features/itpcom/render.test.ts and src/features/site/render.test.ts, and their golden JSON.
- src/features/photos/photos.test.ts.
- tests/e2e/08-delivery7.spec.ts.
- scripts/db-check.ts.

Risks: The migration cannot be only generated. A generated migration that swaps the unique key for (project_id, number) fails on every seeded or dev database, because HLT-026 has a P-01 in both registers. The renumbering and backfills need a custom migration, ordered between the column additions and the constraint swap. CLAUDE.md forbids editing an existing migration, so this means a custom migration plus a generated one (precedent: 0012), not editing a generated file by hand. · Photo access would leak if photos are looked up by number only. photoGate (src/features/photos/access.ts) uses clientSeesPhotos and photoScreenOf from the request's `register` parameter. If the lookup ignores the register, a client sending register=itp for P-06 (a site item) would see internal site photos. The origin must come from the database row. · The client's export would leak NCR numbers. The ITP export (src/server/export/providers/control.ts itpWorkbook) writes `ncrNumber` with no viewer check, and the client may export the itp screen. Once site items with NCR-029 join the list, the client's export would show NCR numbers, against P-10b, unless they are masked. · The triage says 'P-09, P-10…' for new numbers. demoFloor(tx, 9) in addPunchItem keeps demo additions at P-10 (the prototype's 'P-'+(10+i)), so in demo mode the next number after P-08 is P-10, not P-09. The golden and DAL tests expect P-10. · The new `system` column chosen from a list goes beyond A-6 and the prototype. AF.punch has a free-text 'System / location' field and Blocking A uses the location's first-word prefix. Replacing that is a refinement nobody asked for, and site items cannot be backfilled from their location because it is ''. Prefer keeping the location field and the prefix rule, or record it as a deliberate deviation. · Rights would widen. Letting the Site editors (team member, department head) add, start and close reserves, including category A, reverses the delivery-5 rule that a team member is refused, and quality and the engineering lead do not have the site screen in their workspace. It is a rights default for the owner to confirm (P-2). An 'Add punch item' button on the Site screen is not in the prototype, where the Site punch list is read-only; it is safer to offer it only on Inspections & punch list, which the tablet layout (X-4) already puts first. · Seed data: the two NCR-029 anchor-bolt items (ITP P-01 Closed A, site P-01 Open) are the same reserve described twice. Keeping both, with the site one backfilled as B, means Blocking A ignores the open one. The triage flags this to the owner, and the flag should stay. · Displayed values change. The Site 'Punch list open' tile goes from the 3 site items to all 8 items. The client will now see items of Site origin (with owner party Client or Keystone) that were previously hidden. Both changes need golden updates citing A-6 and a mention in the next delivery's deviations table. · The triage's file list is incomplete. Missing: src/features/photos/access.ts, the photo API routes, punch-step.tsx, add-buttons.tsx, commissioning-view.tsx, src/i18n/extra/index.ts (registering the new JSON), delivery5-writes.test.ts, the itpcom, site and photos tests, and the e2e spec 08.

#### R-057 — Figer dans le projet les systèmes ITP du type de machine (buildable, S)

Freeze each project's ITP systems in its plan (E-9 = a).

1. **Schema.** New table `itp_plan_systems` in `src/server/db/schema/operations.ts`:
   - columns `plan_id` → `itp_plans` (on delete cascade), `position`, `name`;
   - primary key (`plan_id`, `position`), unique (`plan_id`, `name`).

2. **Migrations.** `npm run db:generate` gives 0016. A separate `drizzle-kit generate --custom` migration (0017, as 0012 did) backfills the systems:
   - Each existing plan gets the current systems of its `machine_type_code`, or AUTRE's when that type has none, in position order.
   - Only plans with no rows are filled, so the migration is idempotent.

3. **Writers copy the systems in the same transaction.**
   - `createWizardProject`: add `orderBy(position)` to the `itpRows` query and insert the rows with the plan.
   - `addItpInspection`: when it creates the missing plan, freeze the type's list (else AUTRE's) at that moment, then validate against the frozen list.
   - `seed/execution.ts`: copy from `PROTO.ITP_TEMPLATES[plan.tpl]`, not from the database. The machine-type aggregate is seeded later, in `seedEngineering`.

4. **Readers.**
   - `itp.ts` gets a `planSystemsOf(planId)` reader. It falls back to the machine type, then AUTRE, only for a plan without rows.
   - `getItpPlan.systems`, `templateSystemsOf` (same signature: the plan's systems, else the project's type for a project without a plan), `listItpSystems`, the `addItpInspection` validation and the commissioning `planSat` check and `listSatSystems` then follow the frozen list.

5. **Optional.**
   - A help line on the Machine types « ITP systems » card, "Changes apply to projects created afterwards", in `src/i18n/extra/admin.json`.
   - Fix the stale header comment at `src/server/dal/mtypes.ts:5`.
   - Add `itp_plan_systems: 6` to `scripts/db-check.ts`.

6. **Tests.**
   - After `setMachineTypeItpSystems('EMP', [...])`, HLT-026 keeps its 6 systems, its groups and its « Add inspection » / Plan SAT lists.
   - A wizard project created afterwards gets the new list.
   - `addItpInspection` on a project without a plan (NDC-031) freezes the list of that moment.
   - A migration test re-runs the 0017 SQL, in the style of `migration-0012.test.ts`.

The frozen systems are not editable per project in this item; that comes with R-053.

Risks: Seed order: seedExecution runs before the machine-type aggregate is seeded (seed/index.ts:56-61, also in the Delivery 5 backfill). Copying from the database there would freeze an empty list for HLT-026. Copy from PROTO.ITP_TEMPLATES instead. · The backfill must be idempotent (only plans without rows, or ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING). The repo's migration tests re-run the SQL on a seeded database (migration-0012.test.ts pattern), and a plain INSERT would break on the (plan_id, position) primary key. · unique(plan_id, name) relies on setMachineTypeItpSystems refusing duplicates. machine_type_itp_systems has no unique-name constraint in the database, so the backfill should be defensive (DISTINCT, ON CONFLICT) in case a database holds duplicates. · The wizard's itpRows query (projects-register.ts:350) has no orderBy. Copying without one could freeze the systems in the wrong order. · Projects without a plan (blank template, createProject in projects.ts, schedule import, type with no systems) still follow the live type until their first inspection. This is acceptable under E-9, because nothing was instantiated at creation, but it should be stated in the delivery notes. · Existing plans are frozen with the type's list at migration time, not the list they had when created (that history is not stored). There is no visible change, but strictly it is not 'the model of their creation'. · The help line is a new UI string absent from the prototype. It is minor and optional, but keep it a help line, not a new control. · Out of scope but related to E-9: the REX payment pattern and the plan label (getItpPlan.template, the type's label) are still read live; check whether E-9 should cover them too.

#### R-118 — Permettre au CP de créer et renseigner la fiche chantier (partly buildable, L)

1) 'Préparer le chantier' on the Site empty state. Who: project leads (ctx.isLead) who also have ctx.canEdit('site'). When: project stage seq ≥ 4 (Études). Hidden at Clos and Annulé; Garantie is to be decided. The writer creates the unique site_sheets row.
- Defaults from the project: site city and country (project_sites), contract currency, else project currency, else EUR, and a client stakeholder as contact.
- Typed fields: time zone, language, hours, holidays, access.
- Start and end dates are required, with end ≥ start.
- plan_days is computed on the project calendar, done_days = 0, man-days are computed by the DAL.
- The writer records audit and activity, then revalidates.
2) 'Edit site sheet' for the same people.
3) Line entry through Server Functions with stale guards (next position = max + 1):
- Client prerequisites: title, plan activity of the project, need date, evidence; they start Pending.
- Equipment: name, source shipped/local/client, note; it starts Pending.
- Rotations: the prototype's columns only. Person from the project team, or a typed contractor crew; role on site; from/to; lodging and travel as text; rotation no. No mission orders, per diem or travel booking: those are P5 / Cursus.
- Mission cost lines: name and budget k€ only. Actual is not typed: 0 or '—' for new lines, marked as waiting for Sage X3 (R-074 / I-3b).
- Edit is allowed while a line is Pending. Removal is allowed only for lines with no recorded evidence (pending prerequisites and equipment, rotations, mission lines). Confirmed and signed lines are kept.
4) An empty message per table. Rewrite EMPTY_TEXT to D-3. Update the stale C-3 / D-3 comments (schema/site.ts:13-15, schema/cursus.ts:180) and the EN/FR test expectations (page.test.ts:107, render.test.ts:127-131), citing D-3.
5) Cursus placeholder. One pending 'chantier' request per project in cursus_requests:
- It needs a new kind and a newly generated migration (person_name nullable, or a project label stored in it).
- Its payload holds the dates, the place and the rotation names, with no matricules until R-027. It is refreshed while still pending when the sheet or the rotations change.
- deliverOutbox never sends it, in mock or HTTP mode, until R-028.
- cursus_chantier_id stays null, and the sheet shows 'Cursus site: not created yet'. Option b, the PM typing the Cursus id, is not built.

Risks: Typed mission-cost actuals contradict C-2a 'a' and A-12 'a' (hand-typed values, option b, were rejected) and the delivery 7 default (actuals stay seeded until R-074). They are excluded; the 'Mission budget … committed' tile stays at 0 % on new sheets until Sage X3. · Rotation entry overlaps R-163 (P5, not validated), whose description lists 'la saisie des rotations' as missing. The case for building it now rests on NEW-CURSUS-1 'a' (Keystone sends the team) and D-6b 'a'. Keep it to the prototype's columns, and update R-163's wording in reste-a-developper.json when R-118 is marked done. · The Cursus 'create chantier' payload is invented: Cursus has no creation endpoint, and persons have no matricule until R-027 (NEW-CURSUS-2 'a'). The team is empty when the sheet is created, and dates can change through 'Edit site sheet'. The pending request must be rebuilt rather than frozen, and R-028 will remap it. · Sidebar cost and noise: siteBadgeByProject (dal/site.ts:182-191, called by shell.ts:194) runs a full getSite for every project with a sheet, on every page: about 13 queries plus Vigie listHse plus a Cursus getGoNoGo with an HTTP refresh. With sheets created from Études, this grows with the portfolio. Every new sheet also shows 'NO GO' (R-120: no Cursus verdict) months before J-30. The badge should get a lighter query and probably a J-30 window; confirm with the owner. · In HTTP mode, a sheet without cursus_chantier_id gets NO_CURSUS_SITE on 'Sync Cursus' (actions.ts:126). Its travel requests fail, and the Go stays NO GO until R-028 lands. · done_days has no writer: 'Progress on site 0 / N d' never advances on new sheets unless a formula is added (A-12 'a'). This is outside the current scope; list it as a gap. · Prerequisites need an activity code (NOT NULL, schema/site.ts:65). A project at Études without plan activities cannot add one. Offer the plan's activities only and explain the empty case. · Positions are the row identifiers in the Server Functions (unique(projectId, position)). After deletions and concurrent adds, stale guards and max + 1 numbering are needed (R-010 stale-form detection is still open). · Stage gate: D-3 only says 'dès les Études'. Behaviour at Garantie, Clos and Annulé, and on read-only projects, is a default to record in the notes. · Scope item 'Fix the D-5 comments' looks mislabelled: D-5 (client log via Meetings) is already applied. The stale comments are the C-3 / D-3 ones, and the old EMPTY_TEXT is asserted in page.test.ts and render.test.ts (EN and FR). · Rights default: D-3 names 'le chef de projet'. Including the co-PM, deputy and director through ctx.isLead follows R-041 / P-2 'a' but should be listed under the notes' 'Defaults chosen'.

#### R-119 — Détecter et lever automatiquement les conflits de l’équipe chantier (partly buildable, L)

Keep the triage scope with the following corrections.
1) Pure rule siteConflicts in src/domain/site.ts:
- Load: the same person is on another project's rotation with overlapping dates, or their pool load is above OVER_ALLOCATED_PCT in a week of the mission. Only the weeks the seeded pool covers (8 weeks from 28 Sep 2026) are checked; a week outside it gives no Load conflict.
- Cert: Cursus clearance 'expiring'.
- Doc: Cursus clearance 'pending' or 'missing'.
- Leave: a leave_entries row overlapping the mission. This kind is a placeholder until R-031, the description must not name Lucca, and the code says so in a comment.
- Each conflict has a stable key. The key must not contain the load percentage, or every change in the pool reopens an arbitrated conflict.
- Descriptions and generic proposed fixes are Keystone vocabulary shown through t() with parameters (not dataText). The 'Resolve' links (COLLISION_TARGET) stay.
2) Arbitration:
- New table site_conflict_arbitrations (comment required), with a generated migration 0016.
- Its own rights check: the director and the head of the person's department; for contractors and people without a department, the director and the project leads. This is a default beyond D-6b, to record as a deviation.
- Do not reuse the site `access()` / ctx.canEdit('site') check: a department head who is not on the project team is read-only under R-041. Use canDecideDepartment-style logic.
- Audit and activity. revalidatePath('/', 'layout'), because the NO GO badge shows on every page.
3) Use the computed list in five places: rotation hasCollision, the Collisions and Collisions open KPI tiles, goChantier, siteBadgeByProject, and src/server/export/providers/site.ts (missing from the file list). site_collisions is no longer read but is kept.
4) Tests and goldens, citing D-6b: src/domain/site.test.ts, src/features/site/actions.test.ts, src/features/site/render.test.ts (4 hrefs, 2 'No collision'), src/server/dal/delivery5-writes.test.ts:245 ('4 collisions') and its table cleanup list (l.458) for the new table, src/server/dal/delivery5.test.ts with delivery5.golden.json, and the seed expectations. The i18n entries go in a new src/i18n/extra/d8-quality-site.json.
Not buildable now: real leave conflicts. They need Cursus absence events (R-031 / NEW-CURSUS-7).

Risks: Leave conflicts run on the seeded Lucca demo data (prototype LEAVE). NEW-CURSUS-4 unplugs Lucca, and real absences wait for R-031, i.e. Cursus absence.validee events. In a production seed the Leave kind stays silent. The collision card's subtitle still says 'Lucca leave'. · Load is only as good as the pool. allocations is seed-only (no writer) and covers 28 Sep–22 Nov 2026. Missions after that, such as Hélène's from 30 Nov, are never checked. The prototype's 'Weeks 44–45 · 130 %' is not reproduced: the computed result is W43 125 % for Julien, plus a new W43 120 % for Amélie. · Rights: a department head who is not on the project team is read-only under R-041, and DELIVERY-7 §8 (rights Q6, 'department heads off their teams') is still open. The arbitration needs its own check. Extending it to the director and the project leads for contractors is a default the owner did not state. · Matching Cursus rows to rotation people relies on personId or the name. C-3 (matricule ↔ person) is still open, so with the HTTP connector an unmatched person yields no Cert or Doc conflict. · Cert and Doc conflicts count the same Cursus issue a second time, already counted in the 'Individual go · Cursus' condition (Thomas, Hélène), as the prototype did. Arbitrating them clears only the collisions condition, so the Go can stay NO GO. That may surprise users. · Disappearing with the cause cannot be shown in the demo UI: rotations, allocations and leave have no writers. Only a Cursus sync changes clearances, so the automatic side is visible only in tests or with real connectors. · The seed result changes from 4 to about 8 conflicts, and all 5 named people get the collision icon (Amélie is new). Several goldens and render tests change; each update must cite D-6b. · siteBadgeByProject calls getSite for every site project on every page. Adding queries for the pool, the leave entries and other projects' rotations raises the cost per page. · The authored proposedFix texts of site_collisions are lost, because computed rows get a generic fix per kind. The unread table stays until the owner confirms its deletion.

#### R-032 — Finaliser les liens ciblés et les relances vers Cursus (partly buildable, S)

Proposal only, clearly marked as one:
1) Add cursusEmployeeUrl(matricule) in /home/user/Keystone/src/server/integrations/cursus/links.ts, returning `${cursusPublicUrl()}/collaborateurs?matricule=<m>` and built with the same qs() helper. Update the header comment for C-1: the parameter is a proposal Cursus ignores today.
2) Site Cursus table: add a personUrl (null when there is no matricule) to the row built in /home/user/Keystone/src/features/site/model.ts (readyView → cursus.rows). In /home/user/Keystone/src/features/site/site-view.tsx, render the name as an <a> with CURSUS_LINK_ATTRS when personUrl is set, otherwise plain text as in the prototype. Add a title string translated through the site i18n.
3) Tests: a unit test for links, and a model or render test that injects a matricule, the way delivery5-writes.test.ts:273 does.

Not buildable now: an employee link that actually opens the right page (needs Cursus to add the parameter, C-1), reminder delivery and reception in Cursus (no endpoint, C-2), and any change to deliverOutbox.

R-032 must stay 'en_cours' (or 'a_faire' with a note) in reste-a-developper.json, not 'fait', because the owner's goal is reached only when Cursus evolves.

Risks: The 'matricule' parameter on /collaborateurs is a guess. Cursus may choose another name or a path segment (/collaborateurs/:matricule), which would mean rework. Confirm it with the Cursus team before or alongside the build. · Nobody sees the change in the demo, seed or production seed: mock rows have no matricule, so the link appears only in HTTP mode. It can be verified only with a test that injects a matricule. · Cursus ignores the parameter today, so the link opens the full employee list. Users may think targeting is broken; the title or tooltip should not promise more than that. · The prototype shows the person's name as plain text, so the link is a UI addition justified only by NEW-CURSUS-3. Option a's detail text mentions only the site link, so the owner may not expect a link on each name. · Until R-027 / NEW-CURSUS-2 (the staff directory) is built, the displayed name in HTTP mode may just be the matricule. The matricule also ends up in the URL and the browser history; it is already stored in the mirror, so this is a minor privacy point. · Reminders stay 'Pending sync with Cursus' with no end date. When Cursus publishes its endpoint, the payload will need a keystoneId to remove duplicates (travel requests do not send one today), plus the claim and mark logic in deliverOutbox. · Do not post reminders to a guessed endpoint: every 5 minutes, the worker's cursus-outbox job would mark them failed and retry them.
